## 00000024  (order 2)
- narrator: 16 and 67 = なつみ (私), 47 and 51 = 春花 (私; "私のお母さんが使っていた押入れ" 51:8, お父さん/お母さん = her own parents 47:11-21). Non-past narration mixed with past, reflective.
- time markers: "深夜0時を回っていた" 16:1; "今日は五月六日" 16:2; "明日からまた学校だ" 16:3; "五月初旬の深夜" 16:10; "新生活の初日" 16:18; "この1ヶ月" 16:28; "1か月前" 16:33, 16:67; "既に0時を回った夜中" 51:26
- events:
  - 16 (Natsumi POV): Natsumi wakes on the sofa after midnight on 5/6; a DVD menu is on screen; Haruka is asleep on her shoulder. She wakes Haruka. Both say only the two of them live in this house now; both girls' parents will never come back (16:25-26).
  - Natsumi asks if Haruka remembers "I am a real witch" (16:27); Haruka tells her not to think about it and says "it was an accident". Natsumi says she dreams of a life where "those two" had a little more courage (16:43-49), says good night with a forced smile.
  - Alone in her room among unpacked boxes from her former home, she lists three ifs: those two with more courage, that person with the courage to endure, Haruka with more courage to stand up (16:62-64); she recalls the incident "one month ago" (16:67).
  - 47 (Haruka POV, same scene): the DVD is the one they watched a month ago; Haruka's mother used to wake her; Haruka's mother once said in this room that Natsumi is a witch (47:14). Haruka's buried memory: when her father fell from a tree, his fall shifted, he was impaled on a fence and died; he twisted to avoid landing on Natsumi (47:17-21). She keeps this from Natsumi. Her three ifs: Natsumi's parents with more courage, "if Mom had not become a 死神", herself with the courage to stand up to bullying (47:36-38); "what happened long ago when I became a witch" (47:41).
  - 51 (Haruka POV): Natsumi returns with a syringe of pink liquid found taped behind the closet (the closet Haruka's mother used); label read out cell by cell 『ド／ロ／ー／ガ』. A deafening sound like a huge iron door opening; the intercom rings after midnight; nobody in the peephole; a strong wind; Natsumi shouts "don't open it"; Haruka wonders if it is a premonition though the cherries are no longer blooming; one cherry petal blows in; a voice: "やあ春花、久しぶりだね"; Haruka: 「お／と／う／さ／ん？」.
  - 67: near-duplicate of 16 (Natsumi POV) with small wording changes; adds "1歳年上の春花" (67:39) and 67:46 "殺さないことを選んだら" where 16:44 has "思いとどまっていたら".
- speakers present: なつみ, 春花, unnamed visitor at the door (called by Haruka おとうさん)

## 000001DB  (order 4)
- narrator: なつみ (私); past narration mixed with non-past, introspective, self-deprecating
- time markers: "今日は入学式だけの午前授業" 15:2; "オレンジ色の夕日が部屋を染める時間" 15:5; "四月になったばかり" 15:11; "去年も、一昨年も" 15:54; "おととい" 15:56; "四月上旬" 15:60; "もう夕飯の時間" 15:82; "来週" 15:103; "物心ついたころから" 15:141; "10年ほど" 15:152
- events:
  - 15: Natsumi, after turning down Haruka and others' invitation to the cherry-blossom festival, lies face down on her bed after the entrance-ceremony half day. She reflects that for her a 桜祭り is suicide and that she is different from people stressed by spring.
  - The intercom: Haruka (short hair, high-school girl, lives next door) brings takoyaki as a get-well gift; Goto ate everywhere at the stalls and said 『作戦開始です！』 again; Haruka's stomach has been bad since yesterday. Haruka leaves quickly.
  - Natsumi explains 四月病 (Haruka's name for her cherry-season depression): she physically cannot tolerate 桜.
  - Dream: beside a cherry tree someone struggles and sprays blood; everyone watches; "I" was looking at a balloon; the person says something: し／に／が.
  - Mother calls her to dinner; father is on a sudden business trip to Hokkaido until next week (father = engineer of mobile phone equipment). A cherry petal is on a takoyaki; Natsumi eats it anyway and retches in the toilet. Mother sits on the sofa gripping her ten-year-old flip phone, fidgety, says nothing is wrong; offers okayu.
  - Natsumi volunteers to clean the bath ("3分以上の掃除は禁止よ！"). Second dream: someone lies under a cherry tree in the petal storm, someone she knows, unrecognisable.
  - 21: shorter variant of the opening (布団 instead of ベッド, "中央公園の桜祭り"; "手土産を持ってきたのは初めて"; "理由は、分からない"). 30: longer variant of the opening, ends at the 四月病 explanation.
- speakers present: なつみ, 春花, お母さん (Natsumi's mother), unnamed person in dream (reported speech only)

## 000001DD  (order 5)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "普段の起床時間より30分早かった" 11:8; "来週末には見ごろ" 11:13 (TV); "昨日の野球" 11:17; "本日休校" 11:33; "おととい" 11:46; "8時過ぎ" 11:55; "この前、進級祝い" 11:58; "去年春花と見に行った映画" 11:137
- events:
  - Natsumi wakes to police sirens in 元木町. TV: cherry-blossom news; she changes the channel. Her mother appears unusually sleepy; her phone battery is nearly dead.
  - School mail from homeroom teacher 向井 to 2年2組: police asked that students stay home; school closed today.
  - Mother tells Natsumi to spend the day with Haruka next door; leaves for work after 8. Natsumi planned to watch a newly arrived DVD alone. Mother calls: she forgot the garbage.
  - Natsumi's door hits Haruka's mother (おばさん); they take garbage down together. Natsumi's inner commentary on おばさん's revealing outfit and "おばさん臭い" manner; she and Natsumi's mother (茜) are childhood friends, same age, which is why they are neighbours. おばさん's age is masked "生まれて××年".
  - おばさん threw out spoiled food from her fridge; Haruka has a weak stomach; Natsumi notes Haruka gets food poisoning about once a year, was sick two days ago and still yesterday.
  - おばさん asks Natsumi to keep Haruka company (lunch money left for Haruka, black tea at home). Natsumi takes chocolate snacks, さきいか (her father's snack) and the uncut DVD of a film she and Haruka saw last year. The door hits her this time; she enters Haruka's home as おばさん leaves for work.
- speakers present: なつみ, お母さん (Natsumi's mother, 茜), 向井 (mail only), おばさん (Haruka's mother), unnamed TV announcers

## 000001DF  (order 6)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "午前8:30" 11:72; "――3時間後" 11:78; "四月八日午前5時頃" 11:125 (news); "今日帰り遅くなる" 11:154; "今夜" 11:156
- events:
  - Haruka greets Natsumi ("四月病のお嬢さん"); she makes French tea; mock ノリツッコミ about さきいか with tea; they start the DVD (laptop to TV).
  - They compare school-closure mails: Haruka's class mail says "stay inside for safety"; Haruka's mother explained classes mail separately to spread out inquiries. They guess at terrorism, a fugitive, hostages; Natsumi locks the door and puts the chain on.
  - After three hours of film, Haruka imitates the heroine (『清き悪魔に転生せよ！　デビルジャスティス！』) and the rival 闇の熾天使, says she has a relative who likes impressions.
  - Ordering pizza with 5000 yen from Haruka's mother, they see a top news headline: "佐波県元木町で性別不明の惨殺死体発見". Article: at about 5 a.m. 4/8 in 中央公園 a body; a first finder reported "someone is asking for help"; person in cardiopulmonary arrest; identity will take time; suicide very unlikely. They hold hands.
  - Haruka opens the 元木高校掲示板; Natsumi asks her to stop. Natsumi's mother calls: drinking with coworkers, home late; her phone dies. Natsumi cannot say she is scared.
  - Haruka holds her head, "私がいるから、大丈夫", lays her down on the sofa and replays the DVD so Natsumi can rest; Natsumi sleeps.
- speakers present: なつみ, 春花, お母さん (phone)

## 000001E3  (order 8)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "午前5時10分頃" 8:65; "10年前の祀耀790年四月八日" 8:121; "それよりも数年前の四月八日" 8:122; "数年おきに、しかも四月八日" 8:104; "10年前程から、3年おきくらい" 8:107; "3年前" 8:151, 8:160; "幼稚園まで" 8:150; "昨日から北海道出張" 8:177; "朝5時" 8:195
- events:
  - 中央公園: about 100 m square children's park, cherry trees round the play equipment, a 1.2 m fence; police still working; press and dozens of onlookers. Goto charges into them from behind, calling "古郡せんぱーい！　新村せんぱーい！". Natsumi explains: 古郡 = her surname, 新村 = Haruka's; Goto = her junior by one year, tiny, bottomless stomach.
  - Goto has gathered information since morning and trades it for lunch; Haruka pays. At the family restaurant ザッハ (full of high-school groups), Haruka asks Goto to keep gory talk until after the meal.
  - Goto: body found about 5:10 by a 68-year-old man preparing a food stall; still alive, moaning for help; died before police arrived. Then: the 女ケ沢市事件 in 女ケ沢市, 飯沢県 (neighbour prefecture): every few years on April 8, gruesome bodies, two killed each time, physically impossible for a human, three times, killer never caught (Natsumi's bullet list 8:108-112). First was 10 years ago, 祀耀790. Several years before that, on April 8, a flower-viewer died in 元木町 中央公園: an accident with many witnesses including an off-duty police officer; Goto says it is unrelated.
  - Small talk: Goto undecided on university. Haruka: lived in 元木町 until kindergarten, left for her father's work; three years ago her father was transferred abroad (単身赴任) and she and her mother came back; Natsumi told her the flat next door was free. Someone says 「…………？あれ……？」 (8:154-155, speaker unmarked). Goto: 元木町 population over 40,000 and growing because of cherry blossoms. Haruka stops the cherry talk for Natsumi's sake.
  - Natsumi: her father on a Hokkaido trip since yesterday; Haruka calls her ファザコン. Goto's face goes grave for a moment, then she grins: "いいですねー、昔から仲良しで".
  - After the meal: witnesses at 5 a.m. could not tell face, sex or age, so the damage was extreme while the victim still lived; Goto concludes 12:0 "全身の皮が剥がされていたんです" (bare cell). 16: flayed alive with a blade, Goto's view; witnesses said 20:0 "真っ赤な死体だったって" (bare cell).
  - 24: Goto's strawberry parfait; red juice drips from her mouth; Natsumi goes to the toilet. Natsumi's self-portrait: grades middle, sports lower-middle; her one trait is uncanny intuition = danger sensing close to precognition, working only while cherries bloom and not always (examples: stopped Haruka from stepping on a viper last spring; her mother's traffic accident; her father's train accident on a trip). Her parents, Haruka and Goto know and heed her warnings. She senses she has misread something in the dream and decides to ask Goto.
- speakers present: なつみ, 春花, 五島, unnamed waitress
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 古郡, 新村, 古郡先輩, 新村先輩, 先輩, ザッハ, 女ケ沢市, 飯沢県, 女ケ沢市事件, 旦那, 御意, 作戦成功です, 作戦遂行, ファザコン, 皮剥ぎ死体, 神隠し, 単身赴任, 直感, 危険察知, 予知能力, マムシ, パフェ, ドリンクバー, スペシャルステーキランチ, ゴホン, でゲスよ

Haruka protests; Goto: see with your own eyes that curses do not exist. Haruka agrees, hugs Goto, thanks her. They leave for the Kogori home: "やっと作戦成功、ってとこかな。"

  - 11: Crows gone. Ise drives the three to ザッハ; Goto answers him with unusual politeness ("お手数おかけします"). Ise: speaking personally, not as an officer; do not mock Haruka's story.
  - Ise: 10, 6 and 3 years ago, in the town where Haruka lived, two people each April 8, six in all, the "curse" by paper doll: the 女ケ沢市事件; all victims linked to Haruka's school there, including children who bullied her. Today, April 8, is 新村栄一郎's death anniversary: he died 12 years ago falling while reaching for a balloon Natsumi let fly. Natsumi (then 4) did not know; Goto and Haruka did; her parents hid it.
  - The pattern: the 年忌 of Eiichiro. His family home is in 荒田地区 (飯沢県, northwest), known as 荒田集落, remote, with strange customs, including a 十年祭 (a 十回忌). Haruka and 美冬 attended every memorial: perfect alibis. Haruka skipped today's 十三回忌 (food poisoning two days ago; fear of the curse): "今日だけは、絶対に死んでほしくない！"
  - This time she made dolls unconsciously: Natsumi's father, mother, and Natsumi. Ise: someone placed them to frame the Niimuras; he does not think it is 新村美冬. Haruka was home last night; her mother works at night.
  - Natsumi apologises; Haruka: "お父さんが間抜けだっただけだよ！" "バカかよ！　私たち、今日、泣き過ぎだろ！"; they hug; Goto cries too. Ise: "もう悩まないでくれ"; "元木美少女同好会人気No.1ってこんなにうるさいのかよ……同好会退会しようかな". Parting at ザッハ: stay alert until April 8 ends; he did not tell Haruka's mother about today.
  - Haruka: "伊勢さんって……かっこいいな！"; Goto: "あの人ガチなロリコンですよ？"; Haruka's face falls; "空気読めよ、五島。"

- events:
  - 11: At ザッハ (2nd time for Natsumi and Haruka, 3rd for Goto) high-schoolers whisper that the dead man was her father and "あの3年が犯人で取り調べ受けたって". Old banter returns: Goto "今回もおごりですか！？"; Haruka "バカかよ！　このハイエナ！"; Goto "この自他ともに認める天才美少女、五島絵梨奈"; Goto teases Haruka's curse story as 中二病; Haruka: "黒歴史".
  - Natsumi gets a text from Ise (straight quotes): call him where Haruka cannot hear. Haruka screams: the pizza delivery (a comic false alarm) and leaves; "日本一のピザバカ女ですね".
  - At Natsumi's home Goto helps with funeral preparations ("今の私があるのは先輩のおかげですから"). Ise on speaker: the killer of 古郡良治 is almost certainly 新村美冬; do not be alone; stay put.
  - Goto hands over a note in her father's hand (11:97-120, bare cells): to 茜, dated 祀耀800年四月六日 23時: he will meet 美冬さん on the night of the 7th to negotiate "例の件"; if it came out, なつみ and 春花ちゃん could not live in society; Haruka must go on believing in the curse; beware Haruka's phone; by the morning of the 8th all will be over; if anything happens, never leave なつみ alone on the 8th, let her spend it with 春花ちゃん.   - Glass breaks on the Niimura balcony; wind with petals; Haruka calls "なつみー！" from above; Natsumi's intuition fires. On the roof: a figure in big red robes and a 能面 holds Haruka on the fence. Natsumi kneels and bows: "おばさん……春花のお父さんのこと……さっき聞きました。本当に……すみませんでした".
  - The wind blows off hood and mask: Haruka's mother, expressionless: "なつみちゃん……。もう……そういう問題じゃないのよ……". Ise bursts in with officers, gun drawn: "新村美冬！　殺人および死体遺棄の容疑で緊急逮捕する！". She: "私、騙されてたのね……" "行くわよ、春花……"; they fall toward the parking lot; below, nobody. "春花が、消えちゃった……"

## 0000057B  (order 37)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "数時間" 11:0; "間もなく、四月八日が終わろうとしている" 11:13; "午前0時を知らせる時報" 11:19
- events:
  - 11: At the police station for hours. No trace of Haruka or her mother; no sign they entered a lower floor; fresh deep footprints at the landing spot; the Niimura car gone: presumed jumped 5 floors and fled by car. The cordon came late; Ise regrets it. Natsumi recalls the 神隠し theory of the 女ケ沢市事件.
  - Near midnight she prays for both fathers; Goto too. The midnight time signal sounds like the clock at Haruka's home. "春花はきっと戻ってくる".
  - Goto asks about the letter; Natsumi: "私、何も知らない。五島も……何も知らないよね……？" She fears losing more people.
  - Her mother arrives, haggard. Goto, grinning: "この前の先輩の手紙、面白かったですよ！　でも私からの手紙は、人に見つからない場所に、置いておいてくださいね！" (a deliberate lie, a protest). The mother goes outside. Goto: the letter was kept where it would be found; Natsumi understands her mother meant to make it public if something happened. Goto: "謝りはしますけど、反省はしません". "よくて自首。最悪の場合は――".

  - Cheapest item: blend coffee at 1200 yen, plus a 300-yen-per-person チャージ料. Natsumi answers in flat katakana (ワカラナイ, ワタシ、スマホ、ナイ, ボッタクリ, ナンデモナイ); Haruka objects to the 片言. They order two blend coffees; a free cookie comes with them.

They vow never to return to the café until adulthood.

- events:
  - 8: After 20:00. Haruka asleep after dinner. Goto returns glum: her sister came home and left; they were close until elementary school; Goto thinks being praised as a genius made her sister distant. The café waitress's perfume is in the living room.
  - Natsumi asks Goto to come downstairs to talk; Goto: "全部は分かりませんけど、分かることは話しますね". Expensive tea; Goto asks if Natsumi remembers their first talk.
  - Goto's memory: she joined the volleyball club in May of her first middle-school year (Natsumi in 2nd year). Natsumi, poor at sport, begged the coach for one more ball until she touched it. Goto, who had never needed effort, was curious; she spoke to her after practice, awkward and gloomy.
  - Natsumi called her "五島" (bare): nobody but teachers had used her surname, and they said 五島『さん』; everyone called her 『絵梨奈ちゃん』, which felt like a wall; being called 五島 made her happy.

  - On the tryout day, April 6, Natsumi collapsed in class (Goto did not know about 四月病 then); from the nurse's bed she warned Goto about the 2nd-floor stairs; a shelf fell from the stairwell there. Natsumi collapsed again at the tryout; Goto cried; Natsumi: no regrets; "ありがとうね五島。私のかわいい後輩！".
  - Goto's closing narration: Natsumi drew her out of her shell; "弱々しく見えるけど、弱い人じゃない"; "私は、先輩を……／あなたを心から尊敬します。"

  - Natsumi realises she is bait. Haruka wakes, furious ("バカ野郎！　離れるなって言っただろうが！"), apologises "私のせいだ"; "死神は人間じゃないんだ！"; "死神は、魔女によって呼び出される悪魔だ" (appears only on April 8 and always kills). Natsumi demands who the 魔女 is: "魔女は……／私だ……！". Goto looks away.
  - Natsumi's panic spiral (who to trust); 15:0 "もう何も分からない。" 19: she shoves the table, runs up, locks herself in Goto's room; Ise arrives with officers. The power goes out (only this house); the window breaks: the 怪人 in red robes and 能面 with a hatchet and a metal club enters. Ise fires; it dodges every bullet. Natsumi: "これが死神だ……！". Ise orders her out of the window.
  - She climbs down the drainpipe, falls about 2 m, injures her left elbow; gunshots from the house. A voice calls: "来なさい", "ほら、おいで"; nobody is there; she walks toward it: "あの人に、会いに行く".

## 00000641  (order 46)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "今日" 11:14; "後日" 11:179
- events:
  - 11: She follows the voice against the wind; she reasons that she does not sense danger but hears a voice warning her. She reaches the closed 中央公園; the 御神木 glows a dull pink-blue.
  - Under the tree stands a bright-red figure like the one in her dream: it has no skin. It speaks "な／つ／み": her father. He touches her left elbow ("もう……大丈夫……") and the pain goes; then her cheek: "なつみ……。信じ……なさい……。春花ちゃんを……", "みんなを……みんなは……味方だよ"; he smiles and crumbles into petals, drawn into the tree. She understands he warned her in the dream at Haruka's.
  - The 死神 arrives and stares at her through the 能面; she: "あなたがお父さんを殺したのね……", "あなた、誰なの……？". Ise with a large unit surrounds the park; he names its crimes and "新村美冬！"; it removes hood and mask: Natsumi's mother ("おかあ……さん……？"). Ise: "バ、バカな！".
  - The mother: "良治君は……お父さんはね……最後の最後まで、私たちを守ろうとしたの。せめて、あなたた――" and collapses; the red robes are ordinary cloth, no bulletproof vest. To Ise and Natsumi: flee this town with 春花ちゃん; "美冬は……あの子は……なつみを殺そうとして……". She dies in the ambulance.
  - Natsumi sees her own grief as "滑稽" (she knows nothing of why they died). At the hospital: the Kogori and Niimura houses burned; Haruka's mother missing; the mother's hatchet and robes presumed taken from Haruka's mother, who is held to be the father's killer (per Ise). Later she learns how Haruka's father died. Police theory: Haruka's mother tried to kill Natsumi, the father stopped her and was killed, the mother killed her friend; many mysteries remain, like the 女ケ沢市事件.
- speakers present: なつみ, 古郡良治 (as the flayed red figure), 伊勢, お母さん (茜), the 死神 (= 茜 in this route; no lines before the unmasking)

- voice/relationship changes: 茜→古郡良治 良治君 (3p); 茜→美冬 美冬 / あの子 (3p); 茜→伊勢 伊勢さん; 伊勢→茜 古郡さん; 伊勢→死神 お前; なつみ→死神 あなた; rows added

## 00000682  (order 49)

- events:
  - 11:0 Haruka alone: "やっぱり、言えないよ……" (continues 0000066B:11:52).
  - 11:1-19 Her memory: kindergarten hanami at 中央公園 with Natsumi's family; a gust took Natsumi's balloon into the 御神木; Haruka's father climbed for it, fell, and the park fence pierced his neck; Natsumi, 4, below, was drenched in his blood and has feared cherries since; Natsumi does not remember and still does not know he died; the three parents chose the overseas-transfer cover story.
  - 11:20-40 Haruka and her mother moved to 女ケ沢市, 飯沢県 (a hospital for mental illness; 荒田集落 nearer); mother hospitalised, Haruka in a children's home; she cheered her mother with おまじない from picture books and learned there are 呪い too; mother discharged when Haruka entered elementary school; they moved to the run-down 七海荘.
  - 11:41-98 Haruka defended a bullied classmate 御子神 (family in an "邪教") and became the target; a PTA-officer parent; she told her mother; her mother praised her, told her to use おまじない and gave her the book 『のろいのせかい』 (demon summoning, curse-killing). Haruka denied any paper dolls to her mother.
  - 11:88-118 The method: a name on a paper doll, spit on it, pray to the ancestors. On 祀耀790 April 8 (the father's 三回忌, at 荒田集落) she prayed; next day the two worst bullies were found murdered; police came, she and her mother had the memorial as alibi. She learned it summons a 死神; stronger with 吐瀉物 and cast near a relative's memorial.
  - 11:119-150 At 11 she cursed the PTA president and her homeroom teacher (false accusations, her mother apologising everywhere); on the 七回忌 both were killed brutally. She was frightened ("4人を殺害したことに等しい"); police ignored her paper-doll story; mother and daughter isolated.
  - 11:150-191 Middle school: bullied as "邪教徒"; bullies tore Natsumi's letters (her only support); she made a last doll ten days before the 十回忌, regretted it next day; on April 9 two bodies were found, more brutal. The town (fearing the old cult) issued a 転校勧告; they returned to 元木町. "私は魔女。死神と言う悪魔を呼び出す、四月八日の魔女。" A settlement 掟 forbade moving there before 16; she is 17.
  - 11:192-224 Three years on; tomorrow is the 十三回忌 (祀耀800 April 8); she asked to skip it so no 女ケ沢市事件 happens; she vomited two days ago (she has vomited before every curse but the first); she tells herself no 死神 will come. She looks at her father's memorial photo (新村栄一郎, died at 30, plain-looking, heavy), apologises to him in です・ます, and tells herself she never hated Natsumi: "あれは……事故だったんだ……".

  - 8: Mifuyu sits in a light car near the building レボハイツ, listening through the bug in Haruka's phone; a syringe of pink liquid on the passenger seat. As she is about to inject, she hears screams ("誰よこの人！", "逃げろ！　死神だ！", "先輩！　助けて！") and sounds of cutting and crushing.
  - She throws the syringe aside, puts on 赤装束 and 能面, takes 鉈 and メイス, enters the Niimura flat: film playing silently, curtains drawn, girls' bodies on the floor.
  - A hoarse, familiar voice: a second 死神 with an old woman's Noh mask (hers is a 小面). Mifuyu kneels: "お久しぶりです。大魔女様"; apologises in formal keigo for missing her husband's memorial; the 大魔女 quotes the 荒田 slogan "1人はみんなのために。みんなは1人のために。"; says she heard of the killing in this town and knew.
  - Unmasked on order, Mifuyu weeps. The 大魔女 names the dead (古郡なつみ, 五島絵梨奈, "孫の春花") and asks if this was what she wanted; Mifuyu: she never planned to kill Goto; she went after Haruka because she does not want to let her go: 古郡良治 said he could no longer entrust Haruka to her; Natsumi is "本物の魔女", foresees the future in cherry season and can curse people to death; "栄一郎さんは、それで……！"; "春花は私の娘です！".
  - The 大魔女 removes the mask: Haruka ("ばあちゃんのモノマネ、前よりうまくなってたでしょ？"). Police burst in and arrest Mifuyu ("殺人および死体遺棄、殺人教唆の疑いで逮捕する！"); the "bodies" are policewomen impersonating the girls; one says "作戦成功……ですね……". Natsumi and Goto come out of another room.
  - Haruka sinks to the floor: "私の作戦、うまくいっちゃったよ……" / "実はこれ、ドッキリなんだろ？". Natsumi hugs her and blames her own balloon. Ise thanks them; Goto (おじさん) asks whether the seniors will be driven out of 元木町; Ise promises protection, refuses her cheek kiss; asks Haruka and Natsumi to come for questioning.
  - Haruka asks Natsumi why she is so strong; Natsumi: "1人はみんなのために、みんなは1人のために。新村家のモットーでしょ？". Haruka's closing thoughts: Natsumi's mother's arrest is a matter of time; the best ending possible.

  - 11: The group date at a new Italian izakaya. Akane rehearses Mifuyu's self-introduction; she says "美冬……金井です……", Akane: "外国人かよ……" (V136). Akane gives her a crib sheet; Akane and 良治 are engaged, wedding in 10 months.
  - 良治 arrives with 新村栄一郎: short, heavy, sweating, trips ("あ痛！"), "だだだだいじょうび……！"; he asks to toast the engaged couple first; downs his beer; looks angry. In the men's toilet: "なんでよりによって、あんな美人を連れてくるんだよ！　どう考えて僕には釣り合わないよ！"; "荒田集落の魂、今こそ見せつける！　気合いだ！".
  - He shouts "美冬、金井さん！" (given name and surname reversed), "ご趣味は！？"; she reads the wrong line of the memo: "コーヒーと辛い物です" (both hate them). Mirrored scenes in the two toilets (identical lines). Both drink vodka on the rocks in silence; she is a strong drinker; the date fails.
  - Akane leaves her to look after the drunk Eiichiro; in 中央公園 in November she lends him her coat; he cries: not good enough for her; a twin older brother with the same looks but full of confidence; he left home from high school to become different and build a happy family. She sees herself in him.
  - Two men from 皆上市 hit on her, insult him ("こんなデブ"); he says "ひゃめて下さい"; they turn on him; he shouts "火事だ！" until neighbours come; a firefighter warns them. She praises him; he smiles for the first time.
  - He walks her home limping ("昔、足を怪我して"); refuses tea and runs off: "じゃあ、おやすみなさい！". She: "まるで、自分自身――鏡を見ているようだった"; "これで終わりなんてやだ……！".

- narrator: 美冬 (私)

- events:
  - 8: Mifuyu tells Akane of the proposal; next week she goes to his family's settlement for about a year: brides are tested there for a year; Akane worries, then postpones her own wedding so Mifuyu can attend.
  - Narration: the 荒田集落 is closed, with odd customs; Eiichiro's mother, called 大魔女, is friendly and kind; the tests were hard, at times life-threatening; attacks by daimyo and bandits over centuries left a custom of being ready to fight and survive; the tests are secret.
  - After four seasons she returns; Akane: "あんた……本当に美冬？" she looks tougher; "うぎゅ" proves it is her. Gift: a big bullfrog she caught and butchered herself; Akane declines to see it. Akane: "美冬が困ったら、何があっても絶対に助けてあげるから"; Mifuyu promises help back.
  - They plan children: a spring-born girl 『春花』, a summer-born 『なつみ』; with 茜 autumn and 美冬 winter they would be 春夏秋冬, a comedy quartet.
  - Both marry within months; a doctor tells Mifuyu she is pregnant; Eiichiro ("美冬ちゃん") cries; baby talk "パパは泣き虫でちゅねー！".
  - At the café the staff congratulate "古郡様、新村様" with caffeine-free drinks: Akane is pregnant too. A toddler (桃子) begs her mother for a little sister and has chosen the name "えりな".

- narrator: 古郡良治 (俺), first person
- time markers: "祀耀788年四月八日" 11:2; "1か月後" 27:28
- events:
  - 11: Hanami in 中央公園 on a weekday. Eiichiro eats cold takoyaki; 良治 looks at the 御神木: only about 60 years old yet huge and ancient, said to have appeared suddenly in this form.
  - Haruka (calls Eiichiro "パパ") and Natsumi (calls him "おじさん") spill a juice; Haruka cries for the juice ("ジュースさん、かわいそう"). A picture-story show with sweets and balloons; each gets 100 yen.
  - The fathers talk: 良治 sometimes wishes Haruka were still his daughter; 茜 probably too; Eiichiro fears Mifuyu would do anything to keep her happiness: "何をするか分からない".
  - The mothers return; Mifuyu scolds Eiichiro for leaving Haruka; money banter between 良治 and 茜 ("ごゆっっっっっっっくり、どうぞ"). The girls run back with matching balloons; Eiichiro limps after them (an old childhood injury per 良治's memory).
  - A strong wind; Natsumi's cherry-coloured balloon catches in the 御神木; Eiichiro climbs ("荒田集落の山育ちなんだ！"); the branch creaks; Natsumi stands below; a monstrous sound like rusty iron fills the park; he slips, twists toward the spiked iron fence and is pierced through the neck; 良治 holds the blood-soaked Natsumi.

  - 23-27: Natsumi to the ambulance: "その救急車、危ないよ？"; inside, Mifuyu raves that Natsumi is a witch who killed him; a drunk-driven truck hits the ambulance, no serious injuries; Mifuyu: "春花を魔女の餌食になんてさせない".
  - A month later a letter "茜へ…美冬" says she is leaving 元木町 with Haruka today; the Kogoris arrive as she drives off; the girls scream each other's names.

  - 良治 reads it as a request to kill two bullies. 茜: "美冬は甘えん坊で意気地なしだけど、バカじゃないの"; she has thought through every other option. They decide to go to 女ケ沢 tomorrow to talk her out of it; Natsumi to 茜's parents (外崎家), on the way; the letter has no postmark; 茜 will use a public phone.

## 000008A2  (order 71)
- narrator: 古郡良治 (俺)
- time markers: "明日" 8:2, 8:24; "800年前" 8:60; "1年間" 8:67; "10分" 8:44, 8:77; "明日早朝" 16:35
- events:
  - 8: (route where Natsumi came) Natsumi sleeps beside Haruka; a fly buzzes. Mifuyu is gaunt, clothes worn; Haruka's clothes neat. 茜: "いじめている2人の顔と住所は覚えた。時間と実行場所、方法を教えて". 良治 means only to listen.
  - Mifuyu's box: 赤装束 with many small knives sewn inside, a 能面, a 鉈 and a メイス; two syringes: 10 minutes of heightened strength ("筋肉のリミッターを外して火事場の馬鹿力を自在に起こす薬"). All sent from the 荒田集落. She cuts a fly in the air with the hatchet.
  - Her account of 荒田: founded 800 years ago by war refugees; vagrants and Westerners joined; fights daimyo and bandits; masks for battle became Noh masks; Westerners brought 『魔女』 and 『死神』: 魔女 = priest, 死神 = the dead; at funerals the 魔女 officiates and mourners wear Noh masks so the 死神 cannot mark them; a Western doctor extracted the drug from a local plant; 鉈, メイス, drug and knives = "死神の――荒田集落の戦いの正装".
  - Rules: an outsider bride who does not live in the settlement after marriage may not settle until the child is 16; no arming outside; no aid to outsiders; "荒田の人は、みんな怖がりなの". A "信頼できる人" in the settlement helps her secretly; the 大魔女 (Eiichiro's mother, her お義母さん) opposes violence and has not been told.
  - 良治 offers to take them in at 元木町; Mifuyu: being near Natsumi is torture. 12-16: "魔女……" / "なつみちゃんは魔女よ！　荒田集落の意味ではない、本当の魔女。栄一郎さんは、この子に呪い殺された！"; the twist and the sound as proof; Natsumi's power as "魔女の力".
  - Threat: she has told them the settlement's secrets; betrayal means Natsumi too; the letter was put in their mailbox by the trusted person; a splinter group of 荒田 founded a cult in 女ケ沢; a believer is in Haruka's class. Tomorrow at dawn she takes Haruka to 荒田 for the 三回忌. "私から春花を奪ったら…なつみちゃんはそのハエみたくなるかもね". 良治: there is still time.
- speakers present: 美冬, 茜, 古郡良治
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 段ボール, ドーピング薬, リミッター, 火事場の馬鹿力, 浮浪者, 西洋人, 大名, 山賊, 神官, 戦いの正装, 信頼できる人, 密通者, 教団, 信者, 口外厳禁, 生きがい

- narrator: 古郡良治 (俺)

  - 46: 20:00. Natsumi asleep in Haruka's futon; 茜 out shopping; 良治 has not told her of Natsumi's words. Mifuyu and Haruka return; 良治 lies "ああ、望みどおりな". Mifuyu: she planned the method herself; she put the letter in their mailbox herself, so that they would come and not stop her from attending the 法要 (alibi); the "信頼できる人" only sent the supplies; the 心中 was meant; she gave Haruka a children's curse book and Haruka secretly cursed the two bullies; 女ケ沢市 was a 宗教都市 about 50 years ago, a local new religion, a scandal, so citizens hate the cult and the occult, and an occult-looking crime would steer opinion and confuse the police; the classmate Haruka defended is from a family tied to that cult; the 荒田 link she claimed was "私のデタラメ", though お義母さん told her a faction that left the settlement took root in 女ケ沢; yesterday's threat was "ただの脅し".
  - She finds the weapons unused; 茜 returns and admits "何もしなかった"; Mifuyu takes the mace. Natsumi clings to her leg ("やめておばちゃん！…元のおばちゃんに戻ってよ！"); Haruka: "お母さん、本で読んだ魔女みたい", "私が紙人形作ったから、お母さんが魔女になっちゃったの？"; Mifuyu lowers the mace and cries, then breaks into manic laughter: "お前から死ね！"; 茜 leaps at her (46:151 = 39:5).
  - 23-27: the warning line again; 良治 holds 茜 back; time slows; he doubts and cannot move.
  - 33: Mifuyu throws 茜 aside, injects the drug ("全員殺してやる…私も春花も、もうここまで"), tells Haruka they will be happy with her father "向こうで", and kills her (red flesh across the room). She crushes 良治's chest ("肺が破裂しちゃったかしら？"), spares Natsumi to watch, kills 茜 with the hatchet and presses 茜's head to Natsumi; she sets Haruka's clothes alight from the gas stove, says heat destroys the drug evidence and she will burn to spare 荒田; her farewell breaks over cells as 良治 suffocates.
  - 33:58-75: 祀耀800 April 8. Carers greet "古郡さん"; it is her parents' 命日. Natsumi: "私、桜が嫌いなんです", "私、魔女だから……", "あなた――" then the bare cell "今日死にますよ".

  - Haruka wakes, asks after Natsumi and her mother; Mifuyu cannot face her ("春花に嫌われたら……もう私、もう生きられない"). The doctor asks if 良治 is her father; Haruka: her father died falling from a tree reaching for Natsumi's balloon. Natsumi overhears and runs.
  - In the corridor Natsumi bows to Mifuyu: "私のせいで、おじちゃん死んだんだよね？"; she heard おじちゃん's voice asking her to help Haruka; she will go to Haruka's school and stay by her. Mifuyu is cold, then breaks.
  - Haruka arrives with 茜 and apologises for the paper-doll curse, "それでお母さんが死神になるなら……私、もう呪いなんていらない！"; she wants to go home to 元木町 with her mother; she performs "天使様のおまじない" to free her mother from the 死神. Mifuyu confesses she gave Haruka the black-magic book and tried to make her a witch; 茜 apologises for leaving Mifuyu alone for two years; うぎゅ returns; 茜: "元木町に帰ろう？"; memory lines of Eiichiro and of their marriage replay (8:228-236, 8:274-277); Mifuyu: "私、元木町に…帰りたい"; "もう一度……幸せになろうか……".
  - 良治 steps outside under the stars; he credits Eiichiro. Haruka and Natsumi come out ("お母さんたちの邪魔したらダメだからさ").
  - 8:314-350: 茜 and Mifuyu recall a first-grade summer, butterflies and sunflowers, and a promise "ずっと、元木町にいようね"; "2年も遅れて、ごめんね"; "やっぱり私たち、親友だよ". The girls plan to live next door (the neighbours just moved out). "さあ、帰ろう。／元木町へ。"

Natsumi and Haruka have been neighbours for ten years; Haruka lived away for two years before that; Natsumi only vaguely recalls why (her mother's convalescence in 女ケ沢). Natsumi's father is home for the morning because she told him to, after hearing Haruka's father's voice yesterday. Three huge 重箱 of lunch; her mother brings a big box of sake as a gift. Natsumi tells her father to cut down on drink "おじさんがそう言ってるから" (a white lie; it is her own warning). Taxi with Haruka; おばさん went to 荒田 two days early and will pick them up at the last station. Haruka: she is sitting entrance exams this year; a trip at Golden Week may be booked up.
  - 11: At the station Goto arrives on time, hungry; Haruka: Natsumi told her to come only 70% full. Talk of a one-night weekend trip; the express to 女ケ沢; "さあ、私たちの旅行前哨戦、始まり始まり！"
  - 25: Goto is late ("ぼーっとしてたら"), 20 minutes away; Natsumi decides to wait; Haruka: "大天使なつみ様が五島を待ってくれるってよ！"; Goto: "一生ついて行きまーす！".

  - Chigaya: pale blue dress, poncho blanket, long straight hair, blue-green odd eyes; Haruka: many 荒田 ancestors were foreigners.
  - Mifuyu waits with a car at the station; a small shed by the station makes her pause ("…………どうかしらねー"). In the car Chigaya asks Natsumi to call her チガ姉 and to drop keigo; she is a university student who goes to drinking parties, wakes up with every man gone, once in a ditch; a friend's photos show her binge-drinking; "今の私の生きがいは五島ちゃんだけ！".
  - Mifuyu, short of time, drives the van at full speed down the mountain road; her face in the mirror frightens Natsumi, who recalls her mother's warning that Mifuyu drives like her true self.

  - 11: Arrival at the 新村本家: a 3 m hedge; in the garden a cherry tree centuries old, grander than the 御神木; a big old wooden house; no street lamps, a few houses, "陸の孤島". Natsumi feels a wind unlike any warning or premonition.
  - Natsumi sees "おじさん" (Haruka's father) for a moment and cries out; Goto: "十三回忌が近いから出てきたんですよ".
  - Mifuyu calls "お義母さん". Haruka warns Natsumi not to react to her grandmother. Haruka calls her "このコスプレババア"; she is an anime otaku who greets guests in impressions and costumes; she drilled Haruka in impressions of herself. Later she appears as Chigaya (wig, coloured contacts): "むふ、引っ掛かったね春花".
  - 15-35: On the veranda Natsumi listens to the wind; a young woman's voice calls "なつみちゃん……", "なつみちゃん、こっちだよ"; she sees "おじさん" in the garden; a man's voice; the "ghost" is 新村幸太郎, Eiichiro's twin brother, playing a prank; the girl's voice was 夏菜, his second daughter, 10, with the same odd eyes, who dropped from the cherry tree. 夏菜 pesters Goto with questions and gropes her; Haruka and 夏菜 trade insults; Chigaya and 夏菜 drag Goto away. Natsumi hears unsettling words in the wind: "荒田集落は……やっぱり、私たちが住む世界とは違うのだ".

  - Her story: an outsider bride about 50 years ago, received by the previous 大魔女; the bride test = a year alone in the mountains (山籠), which Mifuyu also passed; 荒田 founded 800 years ago by those fleeing war; brides who pass rank above men. A second test then: kill someone threatening 荒田, a prefectural official planning a land grab, within a week, leaving no evidence.
  - With a hatchet she waits outside the prefectural office; she asks the kind old official for train fare; he gives 2,000 yen ("あなたに、神のご加護がありますように"); his grandson "チャー坊" runs to him; she cannot strike; she returns the money; he tells her to pass it on to someone in need.
  - Back in 荒田 she is praised: the official was reported cut apart "鉈のような大きな刃物で". She confesses she did not do it; the previous 大魔女 praises her silence. She concludes someone in the settlement meant to kill him anyway; keeping the shared secret was the real test. "今はこんな試練はないけどね"; the will to remove threats remains.
  - Next doll: 五島絵梨奈.

## 000009FE  (order 86)
- narrator: なつみ (私); frame 11:0-2, 11:6-7, 43:12; 11:3-43:9 her ghost story told aloud: です・ます telling mixed with plain narrative cells, opened and closed in 「」 (11:3-5, 43:8-9), the body unquoted
- time markers: "10年ほど前" 11:18; "中学2年のゴールデンウィーク" 11:23; "――夜23時" 11:26; "5分ほど" 11:47; "ゴールデンウィーク明け" 43:0
- events:
  - Natsumi tells the 元木町 urban legend 『モトキザクラ様』: a 妖怪 found only in 元木町, one for every cherry tree; seen as a beautiful woman in a cherry-pattern kimono, all identical; most are guardian gods (so 元木町 has little crime), some are evil, and no one can tell them apart.
  - Her story: about ten years ago A子, a middle-schooler whose family worships モトキザクラ様, drives out at night in Golden Week with her parents and sister; at 23:00 on the summit of 元木山 they pick up a woman in a cherry-pattern kimono; five minutes later she asks to get off and climbs a narrow stone stair into the dark; the family follows to a small shrine at the top and prays with a chant.
  - A signboard: never touch, never speak to, never pray to this モトキザクラ様. Going down, the stairs never end; the summit stays a few steps above; a new board: "降りた階段、決して登るべからず／登った階段、決して降りるべからず".
  - The woman passes; her kimono brushes A子's hand; they plead and chant; the prohibitions return as bare cells; "あなたたちは私のお友達"; eyes cover her face and the pattern turns to blood; they run down the stairs they climbed; the rule is spelled one character per cell.
  - After Golden Week the car is found on the summit, the four missing; beside it four oddly grown cherry trees and their clothes. Moral: never speak to, touch or pray to her; pray silently in your heart.
  - Haruka asks how this links to Mifuyu's story; Natsumi improvises an answer.
- speakers present: なつみ, 春花; in the story: A子, her father, her mother, her sister (voices partly unattributed), モトキザクラ様
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 都市伝説, 妖怪, 守護神, 悪霊, 桜柄, 着物, 元木山山頂, A子, 祠, 立札, べからず, ご利益, 石段, 開手, タレサロ・コニラク・サハン・イウユ・キンケ
- voice/relationship changes: none
- WITHHELD (author): 11:76, 27:1-2 the chant "タレサロ・コニラク・サハン・イウユ・キンケ" read backwards is "ケンキユウイン ハ サクラニ コロサレタ" (研究員はサクラに殺された); the text never remarks on it (V183). 43:2 what the four cherry trees are: implied, not stated.
- NOTE (structural): 11:84-87 and 11:105-106 signboard text as bare cells. 15:0 and 31:0 bare cells with leading U+3000 runs (2 and 4; keep); 23:0 the same kind of line with no indent; 31:1 one leading U+3000. 39:0-16 "登った階段、決して降りるべからず。" one character per cell, 17 cells (V184). 11:41, 27:1-2 continued cells with a leading U+3000 (keep). 11:70, 11:81, 11:102 " 祠 " ruby-residue spaces (drop). 11:26, 11:47, 11:64, 11:92, 43:0 ――-led cells. 11:34, 11:40-41, 11:58, 11:65-66, 11:72-73, 11:88-89 family voices, some unattributed.

## 00000A18  (order 87)

- time markers: "東京の大学に入って" 11:7; "引っ越しの当日" 11:73; "その夜" 11:108; "夜の3時" 11:114; "築年数が80年" 11:51
- events:
  - Chigaya's doll is drawn last; her speech is back to normal. Her story: flat-hunting alone in Tokyo, a street fortune-teller calls her "オッドアイのお姉さん" and reads her face for free: never make an easy choice; someone close to her lies and manipulates everyone; it may cost her life.
  - She finds a flat 10 minutes from university and station, rent 20,000 yen, the building 80 years old but renovated; she senses no one living there; she takes room 205 (204 and 206 beside it, 206 the end room); the building is 『詩林館』.
  - Moving day: she knocks at 204 with a gift of manju; a knock answers; she opens it herself; a gaunt, wild-eyed young woman in a ruined, unrenovated room takes the gift: "後で行きます……". A web search for 詩林館 finds nothing.
  - 3 a.m.: knocking; the neighbour at the peephole, convulsing; Chigaya opens; "やっと出た……！"; the woman presses a box on her ("お礼……です……") and flees; she has the same green-and-blue odd eyes and a face like Chigaya's. Inside: an old board brushed "死隣館" (a pun on 詩林館). Chigaya feels faint and sleeps.
  - She wakes in a storm: the room ruined, her things decayed, herself skin and bone; door and window will not open; her phone crumbles to sand; time runs fast in the room; starving. A knock; she knocks back; the door opens: her own self of yesterday with the gift; she snatches it and eats 20 manju. At night the door opens; she knocks at 206; her self of yesterday answers; she hands over the board ("お礼……です……"), hears "あなたもオッドアイ……" and flees back.
  - She wakes in her own normal room, healthy, and moves out at once; the other self had no one left to pass the board to. Goto: "ある意味ループものですね"; Chigaya: "あくまでも怪談だからね".
- speakers present: 新村茅萱, 新村エリカ (11:2), 五島 (11:303-304); in the story: an unnamed fortune-teller, an unnamed estate agent (a woman), the woman next door (Chigaya's other self)

## 00000A30  (order 88)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "21時" 11:25; "22時" 11:27, 11:218; "800年以上" 11:67; "50年前" 11:98; "500年前" 11:120; "10年前" 11:86, 11:207
- events:
  - Voting: Natsumi votes for Goto; the grandmother counts: Goto 3, Chigaya 2, Natsumi 1; the teams tie 3-3; "勝者は、なしだ！" (one winner only, so none). Chigaya praises Goto; they shake hands.
  - Goto's fireworks (handheld only); everyone plays; Mifuyu tidies the kitchen. On the veranda the grandmother, out of costume for once, brings tea: the first settler built beside this cherry as a 御神木, 800+ years old; 荒田 was founded by a bloodline of the 帝 fleeing war. The roots are rotting; next year's blossoms uncertain; Natsumi: that is why the cherry's voice, and おじさん's voice through it, is dull here.
  - The grandmother wants to change 荒田: once gloomy and closed, it did near-criminal things; her ghost story was true (someone in the settlement killed the official 50 years ago); the slogan meant helping even by killing; that is wrong. Natsumi's help in 女ケ沢 ten years ago changed her mind; Natsumi remembers almost nothing of 女ケ沢; her parents know.
  - 荒田 made a drug "like a narcotic" to raise physical ability, used in hunting before the war, made for 500 years, banned by the 大魔女 of the time; she has reasons to think it may still exist. She wants 荒田 to live by the law and trust; thanks Natsumi.
  - Goto calls her "エリカちゃん"; she offers 絵梨奈ちゃん; Goto prefers 五島 (絵梨奈 feels like a wall) but lets only her say 絵梨奈; friends, no keigo; Natsumi is made to do the same. エリカ is the name of a winter flower.
  - Something strange in 荒田 for ten years; "呪いを信じるかね？"; "呪いなんてもの、ありゃしない。あるとすれば、それは誰かが裏で手を引いている". At 22:00 she ends the fireworks and asks Natsumi to come to something important.

## 00000A62  (order 90)
- narrator: なつみ (私); continues the block-26 variant of 00000A48 (夏菜 not present)
- time markers: "明日、祀耀800年の四月八日は満月です" 11:2; "70年くらい前" 11:7; "4年くらい前" 19:23; "10年前――祀耀790年の四月一七日" 19:122; "私が7歳だった頃" 19:124
- events:
  - Moonlit walk. 荒田 has only one cherry tree, the 新村本家's: about 70 years ago some group took almost all; they were a rare variety. Into the forest away from 荒田駅, toward a cliff over a river.
  - Natsumi asks if 幸太郎's wife fell from that cliff; Haruka stops: "お前らさ……呪いって信じるか？". At the graveyard clearing Natsumi leans over the cliff edge; Haruka hauls her back ("何やってんだバカ野郎！"). One crude grave pillar about 1 m high among smashed stones.
  - Haruka: 幸太郎's wife サクラ, from 荒田, his childhood friend, married after high school, kind, no enemies, was killed: "首を切り落とされていたんだ……" (15:0). She had already fallen from the cliff (itself fatal) and was then beheaded; no blood on the clifftop; many villagers had seen her run off crying and searched in groups, so everyone has an alibi; the grandmother chose not to call the police. Haruka heard of the murder about four years ago and suspected the grandmother, "だけど……".
  - Footsteps: a 怪人 in 赤装束 and 能面 with hatchet and mace: Chigaya. Then Erika in black robes and an old woman's Noh mask, empty-handed. Erika: "Bem-vindo, novo bruxa."; Chigaya bows; "Vamos, Destrua a bruxa morta."; Chigaya smashes the gravestone with the mace; "Return, novo bruxa."; Chigaya leaves into the forest. Erika unmasks and walks back with them (against custom): the rite frees the dead's souls from underground to heaven ("地下には、偉大な存在が眠っている"); Goto names the language Portuguese; many 荒田 ancestors were Westerners who fled here. Haruka asks about サクラ; Erika will tell at home.
  - At home Haruka shows a photo of サクラ: near-blonde brown hair, clear features, pearl-white skin, both eyes blue; Japanese, of white ancestry; bullied at school for her looks, disliked them; died 790 April 17 as the cherries fell; Haruka, then 7, will tell what she saw.
- speakers present: なつみ, 春花, 五島, 新村エリカ; 新村茅萱 masked and silent

Young Chigaya jumps from the cherry tree (she missed the 法要 sick); touching Haruka's head: "あ、痛！"; Haruka avoids explaining the injury.

Mifuyu: "お義母さん……！"; sent home with Haruka. In 女ケ沢 Mifuyu hears サクラ's body was found below the cliff; Haruka learned it about four years ago.

## 00000B0F  (order 97)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "15分ほど" 11:0; "祀耀793年から795年の四月" 11:32; "11歳" 11:57; "10歳" 11:61; "71歳" 11:98; "50年" 11:105
- events:
  - The rite in summary: Chigaya smashes the gravestone. 夏菜 hugs her ("お姉ちゃん！魔女継承おめでとう！"); Erika lets everyone walk home together because of the yearly violence. On the way and at home they hear of サクラ, the 女ケ沢 affair, the yearly 呪殺 and the suicides (summarised).
  - Chigaya: 荒田's mood worsens each visit; villagers distrust each other. Erika: 幸太郎 was the first suspect; in April 793-795 everyone took turns watching him in the 集会所 at his request; the killings went on. Anyone could be guilty; Chigaya is excluded (killings happened when she stayed in Tokyo); Goto: she has alibis (a part-time job, "コン…"); no driving licence. Natsumi: Chigaya was 11 at the first killing, but she can move like 夏菜.
  - Why not leave? Leaving means abandoning those who stay (the slogan). Police? Some believe the curse would kill everyone; Erika will consult the police after tomorrow's 法要. Natsumi's list: make people believe in the curse; keep them in 荒田; keep police out.
  - Natsumi: why was no one stopped from suicide? Erika: she ordered it, on her predecessor's instruction; sworn secret (to Chigaya and Mifuyu: tell no one). The reason: once, soon after 桔梗 died, there was a 呪殺: 桔梗's family was killed and erased from the register. Goto: not dying in April made 桔梗 an outsider; the old who found they would not die in April killed themselves to protect their families. Erika agrees; she had avoided thinking it.
  - Goto: the recent killings and the suicides are unrelated; the culprit used 荒田's April-death pattern as misdirection. Natsumi still feels something is off; the register may be incomplete.

## 00000B6F  (order 101)
- narrator: 五島 (私); unmarked switch from Haruka at the file start ("新村先輩は、お母さんを追いかけて出ていってしまった")
- time markers: "9年間" 11:72; "10年前" 11:98; "9年前" 11:110
- events:
  - Goto stays with sleeping Natsumi and frightened 夏菜 ("エリ姉、離れないで……"), holding 夏菜 as Haruka held Natsumi.
  - She reasons: the case is human design, not a curse; the culprit plans for every pattern and makes others act. Hypothesis: 新村幸太郎. Worry for 夏菜's future if he is arrested.
  - She fears her own reasoning is foreseen by the culprit; resolves to work backward from the 『結』 (purpose), not the 『起』, since a culprit plans from the end.
  - Hypothesis 1: the aim was to kill Natsumi's parents; nine years of 呪殺 built distrust and steered Mifuyu to ask them for help. Too far-fetched unless 幸太郎 can foresee: Natsumi's foresight began when Eiichiro's blood covered her; if 幸太郎 had the same, it would explain everything, used as 未必の故意.
  - Hypothesis 2: list (桔梗's death month, suicides, 呪殺, the 女ケ沢 affair, サクラ's death) plus the drug: the culprit slipped the drug and its instructions into サクラ's supplies; Mifuyu would have told サクラ, サクラ her husband. Imagined dialogue 幸太郎 / サクラ (11:173-177). She wakes 夏菜 and Natsumi for information.

Mifuyu: after seeing the outside world Chigaya doubted what 幸太郎 taught ("お母さんの仇を討つために強くなれ"); 幸太郎 never ordered them to kill; the sisters did nothing. 夏菜: "私はみんなを守るよ！　私、強いから！"
  - They flee by car; Chigaya and 夏菜 run alongside through the trees. At the foot near 荒田駅: many villagers with maces and hatchets, and 幸太郎: "古郡家の人間は、サクラを殺した張本人。なつみちゃんは……本物の魔女だ……". Chigaya turns a knife on him: "私は……お父さんの操り人形じゃない！"
  - He wants Natsumi in exchange for sparing the rest; Haruka pleads; villagers bring Natsumi's parents, badly hurt but alive, as hostages. 幸太郎: "みんな……ついにあれを使う時が来たぞ"; villagers inject themselves; Chigaya: "ドローガよ！"; in a blur Natsumi lies at 幸太郎's side and Chigaya is down.

## 00000BB7  (order 104)
- narrator: 五島 (私)
- time markers: "――四月八日　午前6時" 11:0; "――5時間前の四月八日　午前1時" 11:3; "――四月八日　午前1時半" 11:21; "――四月八日　午前5時" 11:43; "――祀耀800年八月一日" 11:147; "15時" 11:153; "5月にとった" 11:160; "11歳のお誕生日" 11:159; "10分ほど" 15:11
- events:
  - Aftermath: 夏菜 driven to hospital by Mifuyu; 幸太郎 and Chigaya died on the spot; every villager died of a deep throat wound (夏菜 finished them); Natsumi's parents hurt, not fatally. The drug wears off; 夏菜 screams in pain in Goto's arms.
  - Surgery 1:30-5:00; no hope; a private room. Goto, who believes in no god, prays anyway. 夏菜 wakes: "ママが……ずっとそばにいてくれる夢見てた"; "エリ姉がそばにいてくれて……寂しくない"; "だ／い／す／き"; she dies. Goto: "私……ちゃんとお姉ちゃんになれましたかね……？"; Haruka: "お前もチガ姉も……夏菜にとって最高のお姉さんだよ".
  - 800-08-01, 夏菜's 11th birthday: Goto alone at the empty settlement, sunflowers and 茅萱 flowers at the secret base; "チガ姉も私のお姉さんだよね".
  - Her hypotheses: Natsumi's foresight from Eiichiro's blood; the drug ドローガ; the rare 荒田 cherries taken by some group; the dying tree at the 新村本家; サクラ's severed head: all link the April deaths, 桔梗, the suicides. Doubts: why Chigaya and Mifuyu suspected 幸太郎 on so little; something happened to Eiichiro long ago, and to サクラ; 幸太郎 never said "kill" at the station and wanted Natsumi alive.
  - A roar; the trees die in ten minutes; the cherry at the 新村本家 blooms out of season. A woman behind her: "あなたが五島絵梨奈さんね。生前の夏菜がとてもお世話になったそうで"; "おめでとう五島絵梨奈さん。あなたには神使の資格があるの". Goto takes her for 新村サクラ from a photo; she is surrounded. (this route ends)

  - A landslide blocks the path; 夏菜 climbs it with the luggage to fetch help. Chigaya: 夏菜 can hunt a bear alone. A late-blooming 沈丁花: its flower language 不死、不滅、永遠; サクラ's favourite flower.

## 00000C21  (order 108)
- narrator: なつみ (私)

  - Goto says "Que drogaなところ"; Chigaya: ドローガ is the 荒田 drug that lets a person use 100% of their strength, first made 500 years ago (details summarised).
  - Goto: no proof against 幸太郎, only circumstance; "幸太郎さんをどうしても犯人にしたいっていう何者かの意図"; the police have not been told; she sees the background but will not say; villagers' accounts will contradict each other slightly. 夏菜 returns: a ladder is up.

## 00000C68  (order 111)

## 00000CE3  (order 115)

- time markers: "10年前" 11:45, 11:55, 11:197; "四月三日までに" 11:33; "あと1週間" 11:34; "期限まであと3日" 11:85; "四月三日" 11:95; "その夜" 11:49, 11:110; "次の日から" 11:61; "明日" 11:207-210
- events:
  - Summary: they left Natsumi at home in 790; Mifuyu asked them to kill Haruka's two bullies; "あのおぞましい事件"; after each memorial more killing orders; told to stay in 元木町; the girls' letters let Mifuyu know where they were; Mifuyu came back to 元木町 (shunned in 女ケ沢市, once a religious city), to watch them.
  - Mifuyu orders a bug built into Haruka's and Natsumi's phones (Goto is curious and might notice an app), by April 3 as a moving-up gift; one for Goto's phone too, same model; leaves 300,000 yen. 良治 rages ("やっぱり、10年前のことは間違いだった！").
  - 茜 reacts flatly. 良治 asks the software section chief 近藤 how long a built-in bug would take: over a month; a bugging app would be simpler.
  - April 3: Mifuyu is pleased with the live audio; 良治 set Haruka's phone up with a hidden bugging app and remote GPS control; "すまんがこの期限ではこれが限界だ". That night he drinks, vomits; 茜 proposes taking Haruka back and fleeing 元木町; he agrees.
  - His trick: an old battery (lasts about a day) and 10 g of insulating metal in Haruka's phone, so a bug would be suspected; a hint to Haruka, Natsumi and the genius girl. An imagined exchange: Goto weighs Haruka's phone (160 g), says "盗聴器ですね", Haruka suspects her mother (11:153-185).
  - To 茜: "お前は俺にとって、最高の妻だよ". He writes down what he will do tomorrow: meet Mifuyu and negotiate for Haruka; "もしかしたら、俺の命も危ないのかもしれないが……". Last cells "しかし……。／一体誰が……。"
- speakers present: 古郡良治, 新村美冬, 茜, 近藤 (情報部の課長), 春花 and 五島 (11:153-185, imagined)
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 課長, 近藤, 開発部, デバイスドライバ, やけ酒, 30万円
- voice/relationship changes: 古郡良治↔近藤 古郡課長 / 近藤課長; 古郡良治→茜 お前, "最高の妻"; rows added
- WITHHELD (author): 11:3 "あのおぞましい事件" not described. 11:152 "俺が目論んでいるのは――" cut off (spelled out at 11:186-196). 11:219-220 "しかし……。／一体誰が……。": what he wonders about is unsaid.
- NOTE (structural): 11:153-185 a Haruka/Goto exchange in ordinary 「」 inside 良治's narration with no scene marker; 11:186 repeats 11:147 almost word for word and returns to his thought: it reads as his imagined scenario (V244). 11:96 ⏎ inside a narration cell. 11:142 " 巷 ", 11:150 "筐 体" ruby residue. 11:160 typo (V243). 11:17-22, 11:19-22 one speech over several cells, one open 「 and one 」.
- resolves from earlier files: 000006E0:16:35, 16:48 Haruka's phone bugged, battery lasts barely a day -> 良治 put a hidden bugging app in it on Mifuyu's order, fitted an old battery and a 10 g metal piece as a warning (11:136-196). ACTION: revisit n. 00000B57:11:120-121 (Mifuyu's account: 良治 proposed a bugging app for the girls' safety) -> in this route Mifuyu ordered the bug and paid (11:16-42): two accounts from two routes. ## 00000CFA  (order 116)
- narrator: 新村美冬 (私); 祀耀800 April 7 evening to April 8 dawn, same route as 00000CE3
- time markers: "夕暮れ" 11:0; "明日は四月八日――栄一郎さんの十三回忌" 11:95; "明後日の朝" 11:151; "20時" 11:171; "21時半" 11:276; "5時間ほど" 11:330; "朝4時半" 11:364; "明日の夜までに" 11:154
- events:
  - 良治 asks to talk; in her car at the 元木山 clearing (the sweet-potato stall is gone) he asks her to give Haruka back: he will show Haruka and Natsumi the family register; if she refuses, tell Haruka about the bug and tell all about the 女ケ沢市 cases. She laughs, refuses; decides the Kogoris must disappear.
  - Her plan: kill him tonight, flayed, left to die, in a stolen minivan; a 4th 女ケ沢市事件 on April 8 in 元木町; paper dolls on Haruka's desk; "毎年のように、春花を食あたりさせておいてよかった"; then kill 茜 and Natsumi disguised as the 死神; ドローガ in reserve; or flee with Haruka to 荒田, or die with her.
  - At a red light she knocks him out with a knuckle strike to the throat (a way to stun deer). Buys three kitchen knives, a whetstone, cloths and food dye; takes an unlocked minivan with the key in from the 中央公園 roadside; fetches the syringes from her closet (leaves one); cuts his Achilles tendons; drives out of the prefecture.
  - In the van: taunts him ("今年の死神の餌食は、古郡家に決まったのよ"), a manic monologue on her "犯罪の才能"; offers a last kiss, he spits blood; she stabs his thigh, knocks him out again, flays him over five hours, dyes the bones red.
  - 4:30 a.m.: carries him to the 御神木 in 中央公園; petals stick to the body; "日本のみなさん、賭けましょう".
- speakers present: 新村美冬, 古郡良治
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 戸籍謄本, はったり, 呪縛, ホームセンター, 砥石, 食紅, 地下駐車場, 平和ボケ, ウェットティッシュ, 県外, 高速道路, サービスエリア, ノルアドレナリン, βエンドルフィン, ドーパミン, 性善説, 性悪説, 豚箱, 死刑台, お嬢ちゃま, 脊髄反射, イタチの最後っ屁, 日本
- voice/relationship changes: 美冬→古郡良治 良治さん, あなた, この男 (narration), イタチさん (mock); 古郡良治→美冬 美冬さん, この魔女め; 美冬→茜 3p 温室育ちのお嬢ちゃま; rows added

- NOTE (structural): 11:79 "が、" a one-word cell. 11:273-274 "あーあ、／楽し。" over two cells. 11:350-352, 11:355-357 良治's words broken one fragment per cell. 11:206 no closing 。. 11:257 hormone list (ASCII-free Greek β, CP932 0x83C0).

## 00000D11  (order 117)
- narrator: なつみ (私); 祀耀800 April 7 night, route of 00000BD6-00000C81 (Goto late)
- time markers: "――38分後" 11:51; "片道で40分" 11:21
- events:
  - Natsumi asks 城崎 for a ride to "the convenience store" (really to reach cherry trees outside 荒田). He agrees on the buggy on one condition: she holds a knife and stabs him if he acts strangely; "魔女の話は知らない".
  - At the store: a little wind, no voice; she wonders if the voice needs stillness. Her father phones: he hears everything through the phone ("お前、今荒田の外だろ？"). She calls Haruka (no reaction), then Goto (at 夏菜's secret base).
  - A strong wind and her strongest intuition, seen, not heard, for the first time: "五島、今すぐ、そこから離れて！"; Goto and 夏菜 leave; the feeling fades with the wind. "あそこに何かが隠されている……！"
- speakers present: なつみ, 城崎, 古郡良治 (phone), 春花 (phone), 五島 (phone), 新村夏菜 (phone)
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 鞘, ダメでもともと, 秘密の場所
- voice/relationship changes: 城崎→なつみ 君; 古郡良治→なつみ お前 (phone); rows added
- WITHHELD (author): 11:121 what she saw: described in 00000D29.
- NOTE (structural): 11:72 " 囁 " ruby residue. 11:76-77, 11:98 "…………" cells (listening). 11:107-109 "あ――／こ――／先輩？" signal dropouts. 11:47 typo (V245).
- resolves from earlier files: none

## 00000D29  (order 118)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "10年前" 11:19; "10年間" 19:69; "今日" 11:83
- events:
  - On the buggy back, Natsumi recalls the vision: a cave in 荒田; a man and a woman before a suspicious pot on a tripod; the woman bloodied. The man looks like 幸太郎; the woman perhaps サクラ. It felt like the past: "危険があった場所".
  - 城崎: the pot and tripod are 法要 ritual tools, used like incense; only the 魔女 office may touch them, though anyone could take them.
  - He stops at 荒田駅 and shows the storage shed: "今日……お告げがあったんだ……今日ここに、呪殺の犯人が潜む"; who gave it: "　　魔女だよ。" (15:0, bare). The 魔女 is サクラちゃん; villagers "捧げる" whoever appears at the named place; their aim is to kill the 魔女.
  - Natsumi puts the knife to his throat: "降ろして！". He: "それが呪殺の正体なんだ": distrust, pre-emptive killing; "呪殺の犯人なんて、存在しないんだ"; the お告げ was a lie to make her feel it. He will not leave: 荒田 is worth dying for; widower.
  - A message from her father: "不安だろうが、自分の直感を信じろ。お前に危険は及ばないはずだ。" She asks to go on to the settlement. 城崎: "あいよ、お姉ちゃん！　頼りにしてるよ！"
- speakers present: なつみ, 城崎
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: ほら穴, 壺, 三脚, 祭具, 焼香, お告げ, 捧げる, 火のないところに煙は立たない, 殺害予告, 過剰防衛, 立ち退き, 領土問題, 先住民, 侵略戦争, 土着, 独り身, SOS
- voice/relationship changes: 城崎→なつみ お姉ちゃん (cheer, 19:113); なつみ→城崎 丁寧 breaks to plain commands at knifepoint (19:30, 19:34-35); rows added
- WITHHELD (author): 11:15-16 the woman is unknown to Natsumi, "サクラさんだろうか" a guess. 19:61-64 whether the お告げ story or its retraction is the lie: Natsumi cannot tell.
- NOTE (structural): 15:0 "　　魔女だよ。" a bare cell with leading U+3000 in its own block (V235 family; speaker 城崎 by context). 19:3 the narration itself says the word 魔女 has too many meanings (cf. V214). 19:78/19:79 and 19:91/19:92 one speaker (城崎) over two consecutive 「」 turns. 19:106 father's message in straight double quotes (QUOTE WRAPPERS: curly). 19:34 half-width "!" (V246). 19:58 no closing 」 (V247). 19:19 "捧げる＝殺す" full-width ＝ in narration (V129 family).
- resolves from earlier files: 00000C38:11:87-89 why 魔女 power is feared -> in 城崎's account villagers kill whom the 魔女's お告げ names and aim to kill the 魔女 (19:5-24), retracted as a lie at 19:61. ACTION: revisit n. 00000C21 the 荒田駅 shed -> seen (11:75-81). ACTION: revisit n

## 00000D41  (order 119)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "祀耀793年から795年の四月" 11:188
- events:
  - 城崎 at parting: "誰が信用できて誰が信用できないか。それをしっかりと見極めることが大事だ". At the door a figure in 能面, 赤装束, hatchet and mace frightens Natsumi: Chigaya ("ん……なつみちゃん、私よ"), going to the succession rite, as Haruka explains.
  - Mifuyu stays downstairs to keep watch. Natsumi and Haruka check 幸太郎's room: loud snoring; he wakes, goes to the toilet, comes back ("もしかして、俺を呪殺の犯人だと思って見張っているとか？　ははは！").
  - Goto: at the secret base 夏菜 told her everything; 夏菜 would kill the culprit without hesitation; two knives lie at 夏菜's pillow. Natsumi reports.
  - Goto: if 城崎's story is true he is one of the killers; people believe a "true-sounding" story after a lie; maybe the お告げ exists (hearing the dead in this season, like Natsumi), but 幸太郎 was watched 793-795 and the killings went on, so no; the contradictions are "the background"; "犯人が分からないというのが、きっと正解なんですよ". All lie down to rest.
- speakers present: 城崎, なつみ, 新村茅萱, 春花, 五島, 新村夏菜, 美冬, 新村幸太郎

## 00000D59  (order 120)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "既に0時を回っていた。／四月八日――おじさんの命日だ" 11:107-108; "明日は10時から" 11:159; "6時に家を出れば" 11:245; "朝に五島の新入生挨拶" 11:181
- events:
  - 夏菜 half-asleep: "本当に……パパが犯人なの……？"; Goto: "違うよ". Chigaya, sober after the rite, takes Natsumi down to Erika.
  - Erika: 城崎 brought a flowering cherry branch cut near the store; he hesitated (helping a 魔女) but chose to trust Natsumi; his wife was killed in the 呪殺, the first victim.
  - Erika: "この事件にはね……『真犯人』なんていないのさ": all victims, all suspects, paranoia; she lacked the nerve to hand killers to the police; people will not leave. Chigaya: go to the police after tomorrow's 十三回忌; Erika agrees. Erika: サクラ killed by the 掟; someone who wanted to change the settlement may have cut her head and started the next year's killing.
  - Midnight: April 8. Erika asks Natsumi to bring the branch to the 法要. Natsumi: "今からでも……友達になろう？"; Erika jokes about the Mifuyu impression, a week of practice; "茅萱！　次のコミケも行くからね！"; Natsumi: "分かったよ！　エリカちゃん！".
  - Bath with Haruka: Haruka suggests the man in the vision was her father (the twin); blood giving the power?; the pot is used at the 法要. They plan to search the cave at 6 a.m. before the 10:00 法要. Natsumi finds sending Goto hunting odd.
- speakers present: 新村夏菜, 五島, 新村茅萱, なつみ, 新村エリカ, 春花

## 00000D71  (order 121)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "昨夜" 11:6; "朝から" 11:11
- events:
  - Early morning: Natsumi, Haruka, sleepy Goto and 夏菜 go to the cave. The branch gave no intuition overnight. Goto caught one frog; 夏菜 wanted to talk and showed her the base.
  - A low cave in rock; it matches the vision; dark and narrow, like サクラ's condition. An old mat, snack rubbish.

  - A voice: "誰かいるの！？"; Mifuyu: "春花……？"

## 00000D89  (order 122)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "何十年も前" 11:73
- events:
  - Mifuyu with a toolbox, then Chigaya. They hear the vision; Chigaya: "お母さんが魔女の力を得たのって、そう言うことだったのね……". Below are the 法要 tools; only women recognised as 魔女 may enter; Mifuyu lets them help.
  - Under the mat: a 1 m grating over a shaft; a rusty ladder about 5 m down; a cave storeroom with some digging, a drain channel, oil lamps; a tunnel runs on (a cave-in decades ago; do not go far). 夏菜 may enter once she is 20 and a 魔女.
  - The pot and tripod of the vision, but heated over a fire in the vision. Chigaya hauls the pot up by rope.
  - Goto, Natsumi, Haruka and 夏菜 go on: a waterfall chamber (where does the water go?); a locked, rusty barred door; dead cobwebs; beyond, a brown root through the rock: the 新村本家 cherry above (Haruka). Goto photographs it.
- speakers present: 美冬, 春花, 新村茅萱, 新村夏菜, なつみ, 五島

## 00000DA1  (order 123)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "7時間ぶり" 11:2; "今から70年以上前" 11:71; "今日の十三回忌" 11:3
- events:
  - Bath with Goto. Natsumi: the man in the vision was Haruka's father, since 幸太郎 did not know how サクラ got her power. Goto agrees (if 幸太郎 did not lie): the hidden thing was the cause of Eiichiro's injury, in middle school.
  - Goto's hypothesis: Eiichiro made ドローガ in the base from the roots of 荒田's native cherries; outsiders took the cherries 70+ years ago for it; the storeroom was for taking roots; he injured himself testing it, an open fracture, arterial blood on サクラ; perhaps the blood of a ドローガ user passes on the foresight (Natsumi and サクラ). The 新村本家 tree fails from root-taking; the barred door had no cobwebs at the opening, so someone goes in often.
  - ドローガ is unrelated to the 呪殺 except as the cause of サクラ's death. The 起 is サクラ's beheading. Three cases (幸太郎, Erika, 城崎 lying); none fits well. Conclusion: several masterminds who do not coordinate; one lie: 幸太郎's finding her beheaded; at least one other witness.
  - Goto tells the "意味が分かると怖い話" of the son and the well; like the mother, a second person acts in the same interest without words. Suicides and the April deaths are misdirection. Only Natsumi's intuition can prevent it. "頑張るよ！　なんてったって、私、鋼鉄メンタルだから！"
- speakers present: なつみ, 五島
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 固有種, 根こそぎ, 開放骨折, 複雑骨折, 共犯者, 口裏合わせ, 複数犯, 意味が分かると怖い話, 井戸
- voice/relationship changes: none
- WITHHELD (author): 11:160-167 who the other witness is: Goto does not know. 11:84-86 Goto's "もっと不確定的な推測" is cut off and finished by Natsumi's thought.
- NOTE (structural): 11:142-144 "…………？", "…………？？", "…………？？？" graduated cells (V254). 11:175-181 the well story in Goto's voice over two 「」 turns (11:178 」, 11:179 「). 11:196-198 full-width ＝ in Natsumi's spoken list; 11:51 in narration (V129 family). 11:77 typo (V253). 11:30-32 one speaker over two turns.
- resolves from earlier files: 00000C68:11:56-59 Eiichiro broke his leg hunting -> Goto's hypothesis: an open fracture while testing ドローガ in the base (11:76-81, hypothesis only). ACTION: revisit n. 00000D41:11:217 "犯人が分からないというのが正解" -> Goto amends it to "すべてを操っている犯人はいない" (11:172). ACTION: revisit n

## 00000DB9  (order 124)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: "50年ほど前" 11:83
- events:
  - Erika, Mifuyu and Chigaya leave early for the 法要. 幸太郎 shows a high-school photo of サクラ: the woman of the vision. Few photos: she hated her looks and was bullied; she told 幸太郎 and Eiichiro not to side with her at school.
  - Goto questions 幸太郎: ドローガ is made in the settlement from cherry roots, still, for self-defence given the killings; the villagers know; his mother, Chigaya and 夏菜 do not. A book in the storehouse, written about 50 years ago (the 大魔女 of the time traded cherries for research data): boil fresh roots, one layer is ドローガ; dried crystals are burned for many people at once, with a strong high; the liquid has fewer side effects; injected into muscle; syringes bought online.
  - Who sent ドローガ to 女ケ沢市: he does not know. Goto ends it and leaves, eating almost nothing. Haruka is angry at the theory about her father protecting サクラ; 幸太郎: "1人はみんなのために、みんなは1人のために、だ".
  - 夏菜 is frightened of Goto's face; Natsumi knows that face from the Goto she first met.

## 00000DE9  (order 126)
- narrator: なつみ (私); 祀耀800 April 8, 10:00, the 十三回忌
- time markers: "10時になるころには" 11:17; "15分" 11:189
- events:
  - The hall: an altar with Eiichiro's photo; a robe laid out with a mask at its head like a body; a table stuck with knives. About 15 people, the whole settlement. Three enter: the 大魔女 (different mask and colour) with a flask, the two 魔女 with pot and tripod; a fire under the pot.
  - Oil smell like the giant-bullfrog karaage; blue flame. The 大魔女: "A morte chegou. Todo mundo vai Shibaro o Grim Reaper." A drop from the flask flares to the ceiling. The 魔女 cut their arms, stab the robe, add drops.
  - Haruka: "ナイフは頭以外ならどこでも刺していい". Natsumi cuts her arm, stabs the robe; the flame hits her mask; a strange smell; she and Goto grow hot and dizzy. Mifuyu (quietly): rest outside.
  - 城崎 takes off their robes and masks: "壺にファルシフィカソを入れる時は、みんな息を止めてるからね". Goto: Portuguese "falsificacao", "偽物"; maybe a stand-in for ドローガ, now banned.
  - "A morte foi amarrado. Matar a partir de agora!": the 大魔女 cuts her arm, stabs the robe's neck, cuts its limbs off with the hatchet, smashes the mask with the mace. "Grim Reaper esta morto. Vamos la, ir para casa para todos em casa." All leave.
  - At home Chigaya ("魔女デビュー"); Haruka explains the dead are made to possess the robe, pinned with knives and finished off. Natsumi finds in her robe pocket a paper written in blood: "スグニゲロ". 城崎 took her robe off.

Goto: it was written in blood to avoid suspicion during the 十三回忌; 城崎 saw the villagers' suspicion turn on Natsumi. Natsumi has told no one of her intuition; the cherry voice has not warned her.
  - 夏菜 takes two knives from the closet to protect them; Chigaya, with a knife: 夏菜 will follow the car, Chigaya will fight from the roof. Erika gives Natsumi the metal 大魔女 Noh mask she wore, used by past Grand Witches, as a charm ("それを私だと思って、連れて行ってやっておくれ").
  - Mifuyu drives with Chigaya on the roof: an ambush on the mountain road is likely; on bad footing the one there first fights better, even against ドローガ. By phone Natsumi's father: her intuition is silent because they are safe; keep the windows open. Warned of hunting rifles, they crouch below the seats; Natsumi holds the cherry branch and keeps the mask inside the front of her clothes.
  - Villagers in 十三回忌 dress cling to the car and leap 3 m (ドローガ); Chigaya knocks them down. Natsumi senses danger: "危ない！　おばさん停めて！"; the car stops 1 m short of a big tree lying across a hairpin bend; the villagers slide off.
  - Chigaya: go back, only the 新村 family are in the settlement now. The car spins and returns. 夏菜 joins on the roof; she broke the only gun. Erika (by phone via Haruka): go to 夏菜's secret base, one entrance, guard the ladder. ドローガ can be taken again; Mifuyu will guard the entrance: "つべこべ言わないで…地下に行くの！".
  - At the base Erika and 幸太郎 wait; Chigaya got off at the settlement entrance to watch it. Mifuyu and Erika take hatchets ("春花、荒田の女はいつも命がけなのよ……！"); Erika sends 幸太郎 below. Haruka and Goto distrust him; Natsumi hesitates.

## 00000E19  (order 128)
- narrator: なつみ (私)
- time markers: none
- events:
  - "桜の声に背く――それは選択肢としてあり得るものではなかった": she goes below with 幸太郎. He lights the cave's candle stands with matches; 幸太郎 leads, 夏菜 at the rear.
  - Natsumi reasons: she sensed her own danger at the tree but not the villagers' danger from Chigaya; masked and robed, they cannot be identified, and her foresight needs an identifiable person; 荒田 hides identity "so the 死神 cannot take them" — coincidence, or to block foresight? What is a 死神 at all?
  - At the barred door (cobwebs missing only on the door) 幸太郎 unlocks with his key: cave-ins only near the door. 夏菜 relocks and silently hands him the key. Goto stops at a bare rock wall ("先輩、これ……").
  - A room a little larger than a classroom, jars, fresh pale pink 沈丁花 in a corner; a dead end. 幸太郎: "ここで行き止まりだ". Goto: "先輩たちをどうするつもりですか？". His voice drops ("くっふふふ……"); he lights every candle and holds a hatchet: "必要なんだよ、なつみちゃんがね".
  - 夏菜: "パパがママを殺したの……？"; Goto holds her to her promise not to kill. Goto: how was the 呪殺 done, there must be an accomplice, "誰をかばっているんですか？". 幸太郎 claims he did it all, then that fear did the rest; Goto calls both lies. "五島ちゃんと春花ちゃんはここで死ぬんだから".
  - He injects himself (his first time), fells 夏菜 in an instant, lifts Haruka by the collar, feels no pain (ドローガ), grinds her head into the ceiling, throws her toward the door; the hatchet splits rock and ground. Goto works at the lock; he dangles the key.
  - Haruka stands in front of Natsumi; 幸太郎: Haruka is adopted, a 古郡 daughter, Natsumi's real older sister. Haruka: whoever she is, she will protect them. Natsumi remembers being saved by Haruka once and takes the blow; pain; the bloody hatchet; "もし春花が本当に私のお姉さんだったら……"; she loses consciousness.

## 00000E31  (order 129)
- narrator: なつみ (私); dreamlike, present feel

- events:
  - A black space full of light; no up or down; warmth in both hands. A voice: "――なつみちゃん……聞こえる？": Haruka's father ("やっと声が届いたみたいだ"); Haruka is saved thanks to her.
  - Natsumi apologises for the balloon; he thanks her for staying by Haruka and 美冬ちゃん. He held 幸太郎 down ("兄貴は、僕が押さえつけた"). He confirms, laughing, that Haruka is her sister; 良治 and the others meant to tell Natsumi at 20.

And stay by Haruka, who is lonely and afraid of being alone ("お母さんとおばさんみたいに、ずっと一緒にいるよ！").
  - "真相さえ、知らなければね". She grips the hands; he fades: "春花と美冬ちゃんのこと、くれぐれも頼むよ！".

- events:
  - Natsumi wakes in a hospital room, Haruka and Goto beside her; a dream half remembered; pain in her left chest; Haruka's forehead wound is fresh.
  - Haruka's account: a metal clang, drops of blood; Natsumi down with a shallow cut; 幸太郎: "君が死んだらダメじゃないか！". Goto had opened the barred door; they drag Natsumi out and lock it; his key is the wrong one ("このガキ！　すり替えやがったな！"). Erika calls from the entrance.
  - 幸太郎 hacks at the door; the cave shakes; the 新村本家 cherry falls through and its roots pin him ("やめろ栄一郎！　離せ……！"); Haruka: the roots taken for ドローガ finally killed the tree.
  - Above: Mifuyu exhausted, Erika unhurt, about ten villagers down; Chigaya slowed them at the entrance by cutting legs; ドローガ's side effects (torn muscles) did the rest; no vital spots hit; 夏菜 was sent below because she would not hold back. Erika finds the metal mask in Natsumi's clothes, marked by the hatchet; a strong blow to the heart; first aid, ambulance at 荒田駅; police arrest the villagers for attempted murder; 幸太郎 freed nearly unhurt, pale, raving at サクラ ("お前、裏切ったのか！") and at "化け物ども".
  - Goto: 幸太郎 eased his blow because he did not want to kill Natsumi. Haruka hugs Natsumi in thanks; Natsumi recalls Haruka once shielding her from someone. Haruka: sister or not, nothing changes; "お姉さんなんて呼ぶなよ！" / "分かったよお姉さん！".
  - Goto swapped 夏菜's key for her own house key. Plans: thank Erika; the summer holiday at 荒田, also a farewell for Chigaya before study abroad. Natsumi senses nothing now; the dream feels near.
  - 夏菜 arrives, well; "パパはママを殺してないと思うよ": he picks 沈丁花 for Mama every year. Natsumi agrees; Goto promises to catch the culprit, her face clouding. Natsumi: "真相は……知らない方がいい時もあるんだよ"; Goto agrees. Natsumi half remembers a promise: "『真相を解き明かしてはいけない』".

- narrator: 桃子 (私; Goto's elder sister, 五島桃子, alias 藤野桃子) throughout from 11:21; 11:21-128 a です・ます address to "あなた" (the reader, unnamed) posing two questions; from about 11:129 on plain だ / である first person, past narration with present-feel thought cells; 82 block a dying present-feel monologue
- time markers: "祀耀804年四月八日、午前6:32――。" 11:0; "祀耀800年四月八日――。私の妹は、この日に死んだのだ。" 11:184; "今日は、祀耀804年四月七日。" 11:187; "次の電車が来るまでの20分間" 28:3; "ちょうど正午" 28:202; "13時、荒田駅に到着。" 34:0; "時刻は15時" 34:289; "八月一日が15歳の誕生日" 40:83; "――20時を過ぎた。" 60:0; "20時半――。" 68:38; board "祀耀800年4月8日　23:50", "祀耀800年4月9日　0:05" 227:14-15; "4年後" 227:18
- events:
  - 804 April 8, 6:32: a helicopter over the 荒田 district reports the settlement almost all burned and one woman in her early twenties lying spread-eagled in a clearing; "どう見ても……" (11:0-20).
  - 桃子's address: her oldest memory is a friend of her mother's newborn baby girl, seen at one or two; she begged for a sister, named a 'Lucy doll' えりな, and the sister born when she was four got that name; Erina learned from her and became the genius girl; from high school 桃子 feared being looked down on and pushed her away; Erina died on 祀耀800 April 8 (11:21-127).
  - Second question, revenge: she spent her savings on detectives, brokers and yakuza for information on 荒田, ドローガ and the killer; plan: destroy the 荒田集落 (about 30 people) with a handgun and a gross of rounds; she expects the death penalty; her conscience is a "tumour"; she left her plan notebook-diary on her desk (11:128-212).
  - 豊岳駅: her research: Natsumi was bathed in 新村栄一郎's blood; a ドローガ user is "第一世代", a person whose head mucosa touches that blood becomes "第二世代" (ill in season, gains danger foresight); ドローガ is made from the roots of the 荒田 cherry. She helps a collapsing foreign-looking woman and her odd-eyed daughter: 新村サクラ and 夏菜; they invite her to stay; she decides to kill them later. On the train: サクラ is ill every spring and foresees danger to people she meets or hears, only in dark narrow places; サクラ's other daughter is abroad; the grandmother 新村エリカ died of illness some years ago; 桃子 wants 新村美冬, "絵梨奈を殺した張本人", hiding in 荒田 with 春花 (28:0-232).
  - 荒田駅 13:00: 荒田 is state land belonging to no prefecture; 幸太郎 (like 新村栄一郎's photo) and 城崎 with buggies; she gives her name as 藤野桃子. Riding with 城崎 she draws the gun; he stops for a bear, shoots it; 夏菜 arrives on foot as fast as the buggy and kills the cub with a hatchet ("母熊を殺したら、小熊も殺せ", the 荒田 hunting rule); 桃子 protests and cries "家族を失うって……辛いことなんだよ……" (34:0-249).
  - The settlement: one cherry tree only, at the 新村 house; 夏菜 says 春花 and her aunt are away on the 山籠; 桃子 lets slip Mifuyu's name; 夏菜 takes her to her secret base (40:0-48:9).
  - The base: 夏菜 will leave before turning 15 on August 1 because she will kill her mother ("ママを殺すんです", 44:0); her eyes are "cursed"; her sister's eyes turned from odd to both blue after the first 女ケ沢市事件, when she wanted to kill their mother and was confined below; 夏菜 smells the gun; 桃子 confesses everything and cries; 夏菜 forces the gun to her own throat and dares her; 桃子 cannot; "私を妹と思ってください". Her revenge dissolves (48:10-54:23).
  - Evening: 夏菜 has stomach pains (桃子 infers a first period). サクラ offers to read 桃子's danger in a dark three-mat room; 桃子 theorises a "予知のループ" between two 第二世代; she tells サクラ her real name and purpose; サクラ: "元木町で起きた事件の元凶は――この私です"; at dark サクラ collapses; 夏菜 bursts in, eyes bloodshot, strangles her ("ママ……死ね……"), begs to be shot, leaps from the window; 幸太郎 goes after her (54:24-324).
  - 20:00: サクラ explains: 茅萱 had the same frenzy and was cured by ファルシフィカソ, a drug from the local cherry roots; it strikes girls after their first period, only after the frenzy, and resonates with サクラ's foresight; 女ケ沢市事件 was a human experiment: 幸太郎 needed a test subject for ファルシフィカソ before giving it to 茅萱 and sent it to 美冬 (サクラ had sent only saleable goods); 茅萱's eyes turned blue; an old 掟: "緑の目の子供が生まれたら、速やかに殺せ"; the killing such a child does is called "呪殺" (60:0-64:0). All villagers but 夏菜 and 茅萱 know; the grandmother had 茅萱 hidden in the base (68:6-11).
  - 20:30: 桃子 guards サクラ with the gun; サクラ foresees by phone that 幸太郎 will be killed by many; the animals are silent; 桃子 reasons the villagers are hunting 夏菜 and 幸太郎 shields her; 夏菜 appears at the door, both eyes now blue (treated by 幸太郎); サクラ hugs her; "パパが……" / "殺された" (68:12-72:0).
  - Block 180 (continuing from 72): about 20 villagers in 赤装束 with 鉈 and メイス on ドローガ surround the house; one leaps in; 桃子's shots are dodged, then she lames two; they disguise themselves in robes and masks; サクラ fools the villagers by imitating a woman's voice ("おばあちゃん仕込みのモノマネ"); the fallen torch sets the house alight; they go to the forest after 幸太郎 (180:0-135).
  - Block 88: 幸太郎 found dying; he gives two syringes (pink = strength for 10 minutes, clear = protection from 呪殺) and dies ("夏菜……ママと……茅萱と……仲良く……な……"); villagers in 死神 dress demand 夏菜 from サクラ ("大魔女様"); 桃子 aims; they are cut down at the legs by 春花, back from the mountains; 春花 apologises to 桃子 for her mother; a masked villager hatchets サクラ from behind; her blood, 第二世代の血, sprays 桃子, 春花 and 夏菜 (88:0-92:0).
  - 桃子 is seized by the urge to kill "第三世代" and shoots 夏菜 and 春花 before they can turn; villagers: "この女、翠眼だ！", "客人を殺せ！" (96:0-104:31).
  - Block 82 (the morning of 11:0): she lies on the 墓地広場 with an empty gun; she killed about 30, then 新村美冬 in the burning settlement; one of her eyes is now green; the news names 五島桃子容疑者（24）; she recalls the 津山三十人殺し and shoots herself; a long slow-motion dying monologue: apologies to 夏菜, サクラ, 春花, and last to 絵梨奈; "絵梨奈……／大好きだよ。" (82:0-296).
  - Blocks 215-227: replayed fragments: Goto telling Haruka the 中央公園 body was flayed and is "古郡先輩のお父さん"; Goto's resolve; her run with a punctured bike and "私、こんなところで死ぬの……？"; board posts reporting 五島絵梨奈（15）dead; narration: "五島絵梨奈撲殺事件", the "四月八日の元木町事件", and "この事件で心に深い傷を負った女性…彼女こそが、この惨劇の主人公である。" (215:0-227:20)
- speakers present: 桃子, 新村サクラ, 新村夏菜, 新村幸太郎, 城崎, 春花, unnamed masked villagers, unnamed helicopter crew and base (radio), unnamed newscaster, 五島 and 春花 (replayed lines 215-227), anonymous board posters
