## 00000E95  (order 133)
- narrator: なつみ (私); past narration with present-feel thought cells
- time markers: "今日は4人で1泊2日の旅行だ" 11:5; "せっかくのゴールデンウィーク" 11:13; "今は昼の14時だ" 11:79; "晴れ渡った五月の空色" 11:48; "10年前" 11:105, 11:110, 11:158, 11:181; "今度の夏休み" 11:241
- events:
  - The Kogori parents drive Natsumi and Haruka to a seaside hot-spring inn: Mifuyu's gift, a top room booked before they could refuse, "血のつながった家族で旅行でも"; Mifuyu runs a company. Goto is at a new theme park in 女ケ沢 with her family.
  - Since 荒田 Haruka's left eye has turned green like チガ姉's and 夏菜's; the hospital found nothing; she blames 幸太郎 grinding her head into the cave ceiling; "いきなり髪が金髪になって逆立った気分だぞ？" / "どこの戦闘民族よ……".
  - In the room Haruka tries "お父さん、お母さん"; they tell her to go on as before; she leaves to explore. The parents tell Natsumi everything: the adoption, Eiichiro's accident, 女ケ沢; 茜: without Natsumi insisting on coming along, who knows what would have happened.
  - Natsumi, lost in the inn, finds Haruka lost at the front desk ("新村……いや、古郡って言いますけど"); they walk to a sea cliff; Natsumi thanks her for being hurt shielding her 10 years ago; sister banter: "お姉ちゃん" / "お姉ちゃんって言うな！" / "バカ妹！" / "分かったよ、妹よ！".
  - Haruka knows her mother asked for her two bullies to be killed: "もし、私がそんな間違いをしそうになったら、絶対に止めてくれよなー！"; "人を踏み台にして築いた幸せなんて…"; she admires Natsumi's parents.
  - Goto video-calls from the crowded park, gets swept away and lost; the two plan to bring her next time. Natsumi, remembering Goto's figure in the 荒田 bath, "passes" slender Haruka for the family bath: "よーし春花、合格！"; "絶対に許さない……！"
- speakers present: なつみ, 春花, 古郡良治, 茜, 五島 (video call), unnamed inn attendant (仲居)
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 温泉旅館, 家族風呂, 温泉まんじゅう, 海産物, 枝豆, 仲居, 三つ指をつく, 手酌, テーマパーク, 遊園地, 戦闘民族, 家族水入らず, 1泊2日, 社長, 最上室, 隠れ巨乳, むぎゅ
- voice/relationship changes: 春花→古郡良治 + 茜 お父さん、お母さん (attempt, once), declined; なつみ→春花 お姉ちゃん (teasing); 春花→なつみ バカ妹 / 妹よ; 五島→春花 + なつみ お2人さん; rows added

## 00000EAF  (order 134)
- narrator: 五島 (私; Goto, here aged 19-20 and mute, later about 29) throughout; blunt, self-mocking first person, past narration with present-feel thought cells and self-commands; 74:35-37 a short third-person narrator ("全ての結末を目撃した少女、五島絵梨奈――。彼女は…") inside the replay block; 16:52-148 speeches in 「」 (a presenter, then Goto's prize speech and Chigaya's letter read aloud)
- time markers: "大食祭804から1年後――" 16:0; "――そしていくつもの年月が流れ。" 16:50; "10年前の大食い大会" 16:53; "あれから14年経った今でも" 16:71; "4年以上" 16:81; "祀耀788年――今からちょうど12年前の今日" 74:0 (replayed); "19歳" 46:110; "もうすぐ20歳" 52:46; "この4年間" 52:51; "4年前、突然家を出てしまったお姉ちゃん" 52:157; board "祀耀804年5月21日　19:15" to "19:32" 40:22-27; "――翌週、大会前日" 40:32; "ホテルの部屋に入ったのは16時" 40:122; "朝10時より、予選が30分。／夜19時より、決勝が60分。" 40:102-103; "時刻は8時半" 91:33; "間もなく、10時だ" 91:163; "夕方、私は自宅に到着した" 28:180; "19時より、渋谷交差点で開催します" 28:204; "4年前の桜祭り" 22:49; "4年ぶりのたどたどしい声" 97:188
- events (block order 16, 22, 28, 34, 40, 46, 52, 62-74, 91, 97; story order 74, 52, 46, 40:0-31, 40:32-150, 34, 91, 28, 22, 97, 16):
  - 62:0-74:34 replayed fragments of 祀耀800 April 8 (the route where the Kogoris and Mifuyu are found dead and Haruka stabs herself): the flayed body is "古郡先輩のお父さん"; the 788 search result naming 新村栄一郎; the paper doll talk; the room: Mifuyu with a head wound, 茜 with knives in her abdomen, Natsumi's head apart from her body; Haruka's last words to Goto ("さよなら、五島。お前だけでも、生き延びてくれ……"). 74:35-37: Goto, witness of the whole end, gives up being the genius girl; years pass.
  - 52: after the deaths of both seniors Goto bit her tongue to kill herself in the hospital, was saved, and lost her voice (唖者); 3 months in hospital; graduated high school; 4 years shut in her room doing online part-time work, living only by "食う、寝る、呼吸する"; she declines a full-time offer from her employer (社長). At the local 食堂 a couple call her rude; "あの口、食うためだけにあるんだな"; she cries for the first time in years; she knows her voice is blocked because she could not save her seniors. Next morning she messages her mother "私、家を出ます", leaves half her savings, and goes to Tokyo, "like my sister four years ago".
  - 46: Tokyo, first time. At the curry house 『Challenger』 she orders the 10kg curry (prize 100,000 yen, free meals for life); a TV crew films the champion 南崎 give up with 2kg left; Goto eats it all in 28 minutes, on camera for Japonica TV, phone switched off at the crew's request. Director 鵜飼 scouts her for next week's eating contest; 南崎 thinks she has met her before. On the train home Goto feels "『勝ちたい』"; she plans pace, chewing and swallowing, and resolves to apologise to her mother.
  - 40:0-31: at home her mother is near tears, her father back from a trip, police present (her phone was off); hours of scolding, then joy when she says she will enter the contest. The 食堂 owner cooks her training dishes; she grins again (ニカッ). Board posts of 804 May 21 cheer "伝説の天才少女".
  - 40:32-150: the day before the contest the town sees her off (the mayor with a banner at 元木駅); in Tokyo 鵜飼 meets her; on the bus a fair, foreign-looking woman with blue eyes greets her in sign language: 新村茅萱 ("新村先輩と同じ名字だ"). A Japonica TV commercial names 南崎しのり, 新村茅萱 ('美しき肉食獣') and サイレントエンジェル 五島絵梨奈; web searches find nothing on 茅萱.
  - 34: at the steak house 『謝肉館』 a flamboyant waiter arranges a special 10-pound steak; crowds photograph her; she finishes in 38 minutes, with 22 left, and weeps at losing to her own 30-minute target. 茅萱, who was watching, shows her own record on the wall (36 minutes): she returned last month from study abroad, is back at her family home, and was scouted last week; she advises Goto not to go all out in the qualifier.
  - 91: morning of the contest, 茅萱 gives her a rice ball. Meditating, Goto re-reasons the 800 scene: only Haruka's mother's body was fakeable (a head wound with no weapon); she knocked Goto out, set the fire to destroy evidence of an accomplice or of herself, and died; Goto's hypothesis only. Goto asks 茅萱 if she knows 新村春花: "私の従妹。4年前に亡くなったけど……"; she was with Haruka until the moment of death; they defer the talk. Qualifier: 200g soup-filled 豚まん, 30 minutes.
  - 28: qualifier: knife-and-fork halving, then cooling by blowing in the last minutes; 南崎 26, 茅萱 25, 高野 25 go through; Goto is fourth and goes home to 元木町 crushed. Japonica TV asks her to replace 高野 (doctor's stop); she refuses; her mother, the TV crowd (the waiter, 元木高校 students), then the mayor (中嶋長司) by phone insist; 伊勢 drives her by police car to a helicopter; the town cheers at the heliport.
  - 22: the final at the Shibuya scramble crossing, live nationwide: Goto skydives in from the helicopter; 南崎 recognises her as the "イカ焼き少女" who beat her at the 元木町 桜祭り four years before; the menu is 5 升 of rice (16.5 kg) in 60 minutes with 29 side dishes that do not count; 南崎 uses non-alcoholic beer (hops), then salt only; Goto lets pace go at 15 minutes.
  - 97: 30 minutes: 茅萱 5.8 kg, Goto 5.0, 南崎 4.9; 南崎 surges with grated yam and raw egg; 茅萱 makes an ikura bowl with sushi vinegar; Goto breaks down at 9.7 kg ("もう……食べたくないよ……"); the crowd chants "五島！"; she speaks for the first time in four years: "勝……ち……たい……！" and "か……わり……箸……下さ……い"; last spurt; scale totals 南崎 9821, 茅萱 10221, Goto 10210 g, but after weighing the rice left in the bowls: 南崎 9801, 茅萱 10098, Goto 10150; Goto wins; she thanks the crowd aloud and weeps; 南崎: "私の、完敗……". Something unforgotten still smoulders.
  - 16: a year later Goto declines a job ("夢ができたんです") and flies to America with 茅萱 to eat competitively; years of winnings donated, crops developed for dry land. Years later in Oslo: the Nobel Peace Prize to 新村茅萱 and 五島絵梨奈. Goto's speech: sole survivor of an April 8 14 years ago, the suicide attempt, the lost voice; her charity is selfish, to ease her guilt ("偽善者"); she asks the world to help whoever it can. She reads 茅萱's letter (absent): she blames herself ("私が五島を苦しめた張本人でもあります"; "私があの時、幼少期に父を告発していたなら――"). Goto's closing inner lines: atonement to "失ってしまった2人", "先輩たちは、この罪を許してくれるでしょうか", "ごめんなさい" three times.

## 00000EC7  (order 135)
- narrator: なつみ (私); past narration with present-feel thought cells
- time markers: "――時刻は21時を回った。" 11:0; "満天の星に、少し太った半月。" 11:18; "毎年ゴールデンウィークになったら" 11:51; "10年前や荒田でのこと" 11:76; "12年以上前に亡くなった" 11:136; "今日明日にでも" 11:109; "――部屋に戻ると、既に22時半になっていた。" 11:261
- events:
  - At the inn (continuing 00000E95) 良治 is drunk asleep; 茜 and Haruka talk about Mifuyu's old days; Natsumi goes alone to the big bath, swims, then to the empty open-air bath under stars and a half moon; she wonders about 五島, エリカちゃん, 夏菜ちゃん, 城崎さん and the case.
  - A stout woman of about 50, smelling of drink, joins her; she comes alone every Golden Week, is single ("独り身"), widowed young; she tells fortunes for a living and reads Natsumi's face: lucky, loved, a happiness won "from many branch points of life", with help.
  - Her advice to the dying: everyone is sentenced to death by God, who lets them choose only their end. Her prediction: Natsumi will soon lose one of the people dear to her, perhaps today or tomorrow; "防いでいいのかい？…それがその人の望む最高の最期だとして".
  - Natsumi half remembers Haruka's father (who died shielding her) as someone she met recently; she asks what she has forgotten. The woman thinks she has met her; not in Tokyo; she once advised a young, un-Japanese-looking person in Tokyo "『身近に騙す人がいるから気をつけなさい』"; Natsumi: "青と緑のオッドアイじゃなかったですか？"; the woman: Natsumi and that woman are alike, "『相容れない体質』"; two choices: know the truth and be unhappy, or look away and be happy; what Natsumi forgot is "『あることに関する真実を知ってはならない』".
  - Natsumi now clearly remembers meeting おじさん in the dream and the promise not to uncover the truth of the 呪殺 case; she agrees.
  - Second reading: one of her dear people ("お姉さん" as an example) will lose something so big she may lose herself and never come back; Natsumi cannot help her; the dead beyond the sky (106 billion) cannot help either; only someone "from another world" could give her courage. "当たるも八卦、当たらぬも八卦". "お嬢ちゃん……。／いい人生を".
  - 22:30 back in the room: Haruka says she and 茜 agreed to stay as before; "お母さんが2人いるって…なんか嬉しいもんだな". Haruka's phone rings: "お母さんからだ。…は！？" (the file ends).
- speakers present: なつみ, unnamed older woman (fortune-teller, about 50), 春花
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 大浴場, 露天風呂, 半月, お嬢ちゃん, 人生相談, 当たるも八卦、当たらぬも八卦, 1060億人, 防衛本能, 相容れない体質, 女社長, 長湯
- voice/relationship changes: なつみ→unnamed fortune-teller おばさん (丁寧); fortune-teller→なつみ お嬢ちゃん; なつみ→春花 お姉ちゃん (3p, to a stranger); 春花→茜 おばさん / この人 (3p); rows added
- WITHHELD (author): 11:108-109 whom Natsumi will lose: not named. 11:201-216 who will lose "something dear", and what: not named; "お姉さん" is offered only as an example (11:208). 11:166-170 in what way Natsumi and the odd-eyed woman are alike yet "相容れない": unsaid. 11:282 what Mifuyu tells Haruka on the phone: the file ends on "は！？". 11:135-136 where Natsumi "recently met" Haruka's father: she only then recalls the dream (11:187).
- NOTE (structural): 11:102, 11:156, 11:170, 11:181 『』 quoted phrases inside the woman's speech. 11:185 " 終 焉" ruby residue. 11:46-47, 11:252-253, 11:259-260 one turn over cells. 11:218 "…………" opening a turn (ellipsis-only cell rule). 11:279-282 one side of a phone call. 11:47 Natsumi calls Haruka "お姉ちゃん" to a stranger (V282 family). 11:29 "大の字", 11:25 "アルマジロのように" comic narration.
- resolves from earlier files: 00000E49:11:10-13, 11:196-200 whom she spoke with in the dream / the half-remembered promise -> Natsumi clearly remembers meeting おじさん in the dream and promising not to uncover the truth of the 呪殺 case (11:187-189). ACTION: revisit n. 00000A18:11:14-42 the fortune-teller of Chigaya's story ("くれぐれも、身近な人に騙されないようにね") -> the woman here says she gave that advice to a young un-Japanese-looking person in Tokyo, whom Natsumi takes for an odd-eyed woman (11:150-159); the link is her account and Natsumi's guess (V322). ## 00000EDF  (order 136)
- narrator: 五島 (私; Goto, 15, first-year) throughout; past narration with present-feel thought cells; block 65 a cold, detached present-feel monologue ("あなたが死んでくれて……／私は最高に幸せです。" 65:68-69); blocks 83:81-88 a mock-encyclopedic aside (春画); 117:228-316 a closing summary with dates
- time markers: "祀耀800年四月一一日。" 65:122; "4日前の四月八日" 59:94; "――夕方、荒田駅に到着。" 71:0; "――ほとんど陽が沈んでしまった。" 186:0; "まだ朝の5時だから" 89:63; "24時間、何も食べていなかった" 89:100; "外から、昼の太陽の光。" 202:1; "――居間を出るころには夕方になっていた。" 53:0; "明日は元木町に帰る日だ" 53:25; "今夜から雨らしい" 53:29; "朝が来るまで、あと8時間。" 53:319; "高1の時、1か月だけいた" / "もう5年も前じゃん" 37:205-208; "祀耀342年。460年くらい前か。" 83:1; "祀耀735年加筆" 83:57; "10年前の春" 59:9; "――祀耀800年四月八日――。" 117:228; "――祀耀800年八月一日。" 117:245; "今日で11歳" 117:274; "四月三〇日" (Haruka's birthday) 192:86; "七月二三日" 192:111
- events (block order 33, 37, 41, 47, 53, 59, 65, 71, 77, 83, 89, 99-111, 117, 186, 192, 202, 208, 235; story order 208, 235, 65, 71, 186, 77, 192, 83, 89, 202, 47, 59, 53, 33, 37, 41, 117; blocks 65 to 117:154 are one instant of foresight, per 117:164-169):
  - 208:0-5 and 235:0-18: on 祀耀800 April 8 night (the route of 00000488) Natsumi's head seems to vanish "as if taken by a strong wind" and blood splashes Goto; a figure seen 100 m away cannot have reached her so fast. A flood of voices fills Goto's head (the 00000488:48 voices replayed); she recognises it as Natsumi's kind of intuition; "私は死ななかった…なぜなら――" (the file's last line).
  - 65: Goto dodges the masked 怪人's hatchet and mace by foresight (timings to the ten-thousandth of a second); names her "新村先輩のお母さん", then "新村美冬さん"; Mifuyu falters and runs, and Goto foresees she will burn herself to destroy the drug in her body. Goto feels no grief over headless Natsumi, only relief at being free of her ("私は最高に幸せです"); a long cold reflection on money, anxiety and foresight; she decides to act normal. April 11: the Kogori funeral was yesterday; today Mifuyu's funeral at 荒田; her eye itches.
  - 71: 荒田駅 at dusk; 幸太郎 and 城崎 meet them with buggies; Goto is allowed to attend because she was attacked too. One old cherry tree at the 新村本家. Chigaya (both eyes blue) comforts Haruka. Changing into 能面 and 赤装束, Goto removes the disposable colored contact lens taken from her sister: one of her eyes has been green since April 8. 夏菜 (one green eye, one blue) sees it; Goto lies that it is an eye disease; 夏菜: her sister once had one green eye too, now both blue. At the 集会場 wake (a masked, robed body on the table; no incense money custom) ファルシフィカソ is missing: "今、お父さんが作ってるらしいわ".
  - 186: alone at the house with 夏菜 (her family is at the 集会場 all night); 夏菜 cooks; the newlywed joke "お風呂にする？…あ・た・し？"; "五島ちゃん、ずっと泣いてる顔してるから……", like a young animal that has lost its mother; Goto cannot cry for Natsumi and is frightened by her own relief.
  - 77: in the bath; 夏菜 on school plays and a class of ten; Goto once ate Haruka's whole allowance in a day; 夏菜: "無理して泣かなくたって"; Mama can sense danger. Goto reasons: sisters born with odd eyes, a mother with foresight, so the eye colour comes from the mother's blood at birth, as hers came from Natsumi's blood. They decide to search the 蔵.
  - 192: "怪盗団ゴトーズ" (夏菜 as "副団長怪盗ニームラ") open the 蔵 with a key ring; hatchets and maces; a basement steel door with a four-digit lock about ten years old; April 8, Haruka's birthday (April 30), Goto's and Natsumi's birthdays fail; "なつみ" as 0-7-2-3 opens it. A book on funeral practice (left-to-right, pre-Edo style): the dead are 死神; the 魔女 dress like them; only the 大魔女 can put them down; attendees dress the same; a later-style section: burn ファルシフィカソ to purify and prevent 呪殺. Goto reasons out Mifuyu, the Kogori parents as accomplices, bullying as motive; "とんだ期待外れ". She finds "ドローガの精製法".
  - 83: the book (祀耀342): cherry roots boiled three days; four layers: residue, clear ファルシフィカソ (discarded; "the fake of Droga"), pink ドローガ, sediment; crystals, smoked for about an hour of demonic strength; a last page in another hand, "祀耀735年加筆　新村桔梗": intramuscular injection shortens the effect and prevents frenzy; ファルシフィカソ given to a third generation with bloodlust suppresses 呪殺. 夏菜 brings "男女が大人の身体になる時" (春画 aside), really a growth manual, with a section "翠眼呪殺": green-eyed girls to be killed at birth; after their first period they feel unbearable bloodlust toward green-eyed girls and those with danger foresight, cured only by the target's death; the mother of a green-eyed child has limited foresight. Goto understands why she cannot mourn Natsumi. A woman with two blue eyes (サクラ) comes down; Goto attacks her ("死ね！"); 夏菜 holds her back and knocks her out.
  - 89: 5 a.m.: Haruka: "目の色、戻ってるぞ"; Goto weeps at last for Natsumi; 幸太郎 promises to tell all; Mifuyu's funeral cancelled (the rite drug went to Goto); she sleeps holding Haruka ("お帰り" / "ただいま"). Then a dark, empty house, a bloodied petal; a small woman like Goto, eyes gouged and bleeding, hoarse, calls her "サード・ジェネレーション"; the mirror shows green; サクラ enters; Goto stabs サクラ, then 夏菜 and Chigaya, in ecstasy; Haruka finds her ("人殺し！…なつみを殺したのもお前なんだろ！"); the woman sits on her to cut out her eyes.
  - 202: noon: a nightmare; Haruka calms her; both eyes black. 夏菜 phones Goto's sister on speaker; 桃子 is curt; Goto begs "昔みたいに仲良くしてよ"; 夏菜 scolds 桃子; Goto hangs up; a photo arrives: "役立たずでごめん。元気出して。姉より", and Goto cries. Going to the bath naked, she meets 幸太郎 at the door and pelts him ("ロリコン！…ルイス・キャロル…！").
  - 47: after a bath Goto reasons on the generations: third = green-eyed (herself earlier, 夏菜, Chigaya), second = Natsumi and サクラ; the bloodlust is toward "危険予知能力を持つ者". At the 居間 door she and 夏菜 overhear サクラ dressing 幸太郎's wounds (Goto beat him up: "あれは事故だったんだ！"); Erika appears in twin tails as "五島エリカ".
  - 59: in the 居間 (Goto, 夏菜, 幸太郎, Erika, サクラ): ten years ago Chigaya tried to knife サクラ and was confined underground; 幸太郎 found the 翠眼呪殺 book; three generations (users; those whose head mucosa touched a user's fresh blood; those who touched a second generation's), third-generation traits as a ・ list; a local "αエフェドリン" secreted by spores that sprout in the body ("寄生"). サクラ: the first generation was 栄一郎, who made the drug to protect her from bullies. Goto sends 夏菜 out and asks who sent ドローガ to 美冬: サクラ sent hatchets, maces, robes and masks to sell; 幸太郎 sent ドローガ and ファルシフィカソ to test the cure for Chigaya. Erika: "この大バカ者！"; サクラ: "全ての命は……平等です". Chigaya, who overheard, bows to Goto and vows to help strangers as atonement.
  - 53: evening: 女ケ沢市事件 happened to save Chigaya; Mifuyu's motive for the 元木町 case still unknown. A quiet dinner lightened by the 覗き joke. Rain; a bloodied petal flies in through the window; the woman again ("ほんと、哀れなサード・ジェネレーション"): ドローガ spores release ephedrine, "あなたは、ずっと錯乱してるの"; Goto stabs at her and hits Haruka ("私なら……お前を……助けられるのに…"), then 夏菜; she brings the knife to her eyelid; then Haruka is beside her watching the rain, no wound, no petal. Night: messages from 桃子 (a beef-bowl order; "あんた、本当に大丈夫？"; "電話する？").
  - 33-41: the phone call on the veranda in heavy rain: Goto fears it is not really her sister; they talk of the eyeless dream woman, pain, boyfriends ("キャッチアンドリリース"), chest sizes (バインバイン), Natsumi ("貧相だった"), the stolen color contacts, the grave at 元木メモリアルパーク; 桃子 calls her 絵梨奈 for the first time; "もし私が、あの時元木町で殺されてたら……悲しい？" / "殺した人、絶対に許さない"; "お姉ちゃんが好き"; "おやすみ". After it the woman appears; Goto: "もう、あんたなんか怖くない".
  - 117: morning prank: pizza toast lures a sleepwalking Haruka; the family is unimpressed. Farewells; on the buggy and at a grave the dialogue drifts ("このお墓……誰のお墓なの？"); Haruka takes a call: 幸太郎 and サクラ hanged themselves from the cherry tree; a voice: "私だよ、五島。私はまだ、生きてるの" (117:143); Goto: "古郡先輩……？". Natsumi shoves her aside from a hatchet blow; Natsumi's cheek is cut. It is still April 8: everything since was an instant of foresight from Natsumi's little blood. Goto tells Mifuyu she knows everything and begs her to surrender instead of burning herself; Haruka rides up with the paper dolls for the police; Mifuyu gives up; Haruka: "何があってもお母さんの娘だからさ！". The two cases close with the culprit's confession; 茜, 幸太郎 and サクラ are questioned; Goto's eyes did not change and she has no foresight left. 祀耀800 August 1: 桃子 (rental car), Natsumi, Haruka and Goto meet 夏菜, now 11, who will live at Haruka's; ファルシフィカソ brought from 荒田; "大好きなお姉ちゃんもいるから".

- events (block order 8, 14, 20, 26, 36, 42, 46, 50, 61, 67, 110, 134, 140, 146; story order 146, 140, 134, 110, 67, 61, 42, 46, 50, 36, 26, 20, 14, 8; the route of 0000050E / 00000522, inferred: Haruka shielded Natsumi, Mifuyu burned herself, Natsumi's mother turned herself in):
  - 146 (replays of 祀耀800 April 8): the text from Natsumi's mother; Haruka's panic; the quarrel; "命に代えても、守ってみせる"; Haruka hurt, "無事……か……？"; Natsumi blames her own plea for protection. 140: Haruka shielded Natsumi and fell into a coma; Mifuyu burned herself to death; Natsumi's mother turned herself in (arrested over the 女ケ沢市事件). Natsumi visits daily and talks to silent Haruka (empty 「」 turns); after a year she quits high school (money, credits), takes a night job at an izakaya, lives at Goto's house (the Kogori house burned); in the second summer Haruka seems to say "な……つ……" and Natsumi's narration calls it a miracle. Spring 802: talks with Haruka (paralysed below the neck); her mother was sentenced to 無期懲役 for the 女ケ沢市事件; Goto visits; they plan the 三回忌 with Haruka in a wheelchair; Goto starts to say something and stops (140:256-259).
  - 134: at Goto's home Natsumi uses the room of Goto's sister, who left home right after the case and has not called in two years; Goto's mother and Goto say she is overworking; Goto (third-year, retired from the student council, writing a thesis on unapproved drugs, an offer from 元木大学, a presentation contest in 皆上市) got Haruka's outing permission for April 8. At the hospital Haruka (twin tails by Natsumi) says she looks tired; "私、今月で20歳か"; "なつみ、少し散歩して来いよ。1人で". 110: Natsumi cries on the roof; Goto tells the Fukui folk tale 肉付きの面 and reads it as a mask already worn unseen; Natsumi misunderstands. Night at the izakaya: 看板娘 "なっちゃん"; a "セクハラ20回記念" free-beer ticket for 長さん; regulars' rumour that 伊勢 (on TV) is a lolicon; the "サバイバル寿司" game for students (wasabi and Carolina Reaper; modelled on ザッハ's Russian takoyaki; 0.03% win rate per Goto); the 店長 raises her pay and offers her the managership of a second shop.
  - 67: dawn: Goto leaves for the contest, begs "早くお面を取ってください……"; at 10:00 Haruka asks her to rest ("ああ……迷惑だ"); Natsumi lies on the windy roof; Goto comes back, having skipped the contest ("いやな予感がしただけです"); Natsumi tells Haruka she will visit every other day, hugs her, and vomits blood. 61: a dream of silent Haruka; she wakes at 22:30: a stomach ulcer; her grandparents are stuck by a typhoon in Okinawa; Goto: the 肉付きの面 is still on her face; Haruka has not woken once in two years; the talks were with no one (61:106-121 replays 67:237-269); Natsumi breaks down, then accepts it.
  - 42-50: that night she wheels herself to Haruka's room to apologise, holds her hand; the wind rises; danger foresight; Haruka grips her hand, opens her eyes, forms "な……／つ……／み……", then "死ね" (46:0) and strangles her; one of Haruka's eyes is green; the door opens as Natsumi blacks out.
  - 36: next morning: Haruka, in a restraint jacket in the 神経科 ward, screams "死ねえええ！　なつみ！" in hourly fits; per the doctor her brain waves stay comatose and the acts are reflexes; the doctor suspects Natsumi of drug use (alkaloids in both bloods). Goto reads the report ("エフェドリンに類似"; absent in Haruka's blood two days before, present after Natsumi's vomited blood) and theorises: the substance has been in Natsumi since she was bathed in 新村栄一郎's blood and passes by blood contact.
  - 26: endoscopy at 11:00; Natsumi writes two notes (her foresight fired at the same instant as the grip; Mifuyu's strength suggests a drug); the 店長 visits (麻黄, 葛根湯, ephedrine); Goto: Haruka has days to live; the option is anesthesia, which stops the reflexes but lowers her chance of waking; the 戸籍謄本: Natsumi is 良治 and 茜's 次女, Haruka their 長女, adopted by 新村栄一郎 and 美冬: they are sisters, so Natsumi decides; 24 hours of anesthesia, 48 hours to choose.
  - 20: April 6: Goto brings 新村栄一郎's autopsy via 伊勢: an ephedrine-like drug was found; the 警察庁 began analysis, then refused it and ordered 佐波県警 to stop investigating his death; Goto infers the state knows the mechanism; the limit is April 8 before noon; Goto: Haruka reacts to Natsumi's foresight; Mifuyu may have burned herself to hide the drug and its maker; Natsumi's idea becomes an exchange transfusion (交換輸血); at 18:00 the doctor: Haruka's blood is the rare シスAB型, 8000cc would be needed, her birth mother alone is not enough; only Natsumi's exchange goes ahead (20:00).
  - 14 and 8: April 7, 15:00: the exchange is done (19 hours); Haruka still cannot breathe on her own; on the roof the wind no longer sickens Natsumi and she smells spring ("交換輸血、成功したみたいだよ"); reflexes return; she sleeps at Haruka's bed. 8: a dream of the "新しい世界" with Haruka and Goto; she wakes with Haruka's hand on her cheek: on 祀耀802 April 8, early morning, two years to the day after the 元木町事件, Haruka says "お……はよう……". April 30: the 三回忌 grave visit, Haruka in a 能面 (a 新村 custom), at a grave built at Natsumi's mother's wish for Mifuyu (buried at 荒田; 両墓制); Haruka's eye stays green (no exchange for her); her 20th birthday; Natsumi reflects on her own 肉付きの面; "…………／お姉ちゃん"; "私、来年の桜の季節までに歩けるようになるから。／約束する".

## 00000F0D  (order 138)
- narrator: 新村夏菜 (私; 14 in 祀耀804, 16 at the end) in blocks 8-50, 62, 68, 74, 173:32-37 and 179; 伊勢 (俺) narrates block 56 (unmarked switch at 56:2 "俺は彼女の宿から出て", V370); 173:7-9 and 173:26-31 are replayed Natsumi narration (私) from 00000538 / 0000057B; past narration with many present-feel thought cells; Kana's voice lively, self-mocking, sly asides, then a cold reasoning spiral (50:29-70) and a despairing monologue addressed to Papa (62:59-107); 44:184-242 a foresight vision told as present events; 179:150-197 the slaying in short cells, present feel
- time markers: "もう、春だもんね" 8:90; "時間はもう、15時" 14:89; "皆上市から元木町までは、高速道路で2時間ほど" 14:103; "時間は16時" 14:208; "4年前に凶悪な事件が発生し" 14:176; "陽が傾いてきている" 26:0; "元木警察署に着く頃にはすっかり陽が落ちていた" 32:0; "今年で20歳" 38:151, 56:17; "今日の21時前に元木駅に着いて" 44:47; "本当は今日がお父さんの命日でしたけど" 44:46; "現在の時刻は20:30" 179:96; "到着時刻は20:40" 179:111; "あと20分で元木駅に着く" 56:73; "明日は四月八日" 56:157; "20:50頃" 62:21; "20:30頃" 56:240; "祀耀805年八月一日（元木駅集団発狂殺人事件から1年4か月後）" 68:0; "祀耀804年四月八日に発生した、元木駅での集団発狂殺人事件" 68:53; "3日前の七月二九日" 68:239; "今日で16歳" 68:190; "きっちり2年後の八月一日" 74:149; "あれから4年の月日が流れた" 173:34
- events (block order 8, 14, 20, 26, 32, 38, 44, 50, 56, 62, 68, 74, 173, 179; story order 173:0-31 (replays of 祀耀800), 173:32-37, 8-50, 179:0-114, 179:115-197, 56, 62, 68, 74; a 祀耀804 route in which 新村美冬 and 春花 vanished from the roof in 800 and hide at 荒田):
  - 8: 夏菜 runs away from 荒田 by train with 100,394 yen and two knives, fleeing "ママを殺したくなるという…衝動"; 『あれ』 came early, so she left about four months before her 15th birthday (August 1). At さわ皆上駅 (皆上市) she follows police cars to crowds cheering the Emperor and Empress; a man of about 50 picks her up; she plays along and gives a false name, 古村秋菜, 14, from 飯沢県, with a made-up mixed ancestry; he is a plainclothes officer and takes her to 佐波県警本部 as a runaway. She asks to speak only to Papa.
  - 14: 伊勢大二郎 (元木警察署 警部補, director on the 佐波県青少年健全育成委員) arrives, flustered by her looks; she claims to live in 元木町; he drives her there himself; the 高速 / 拘束 mix-up (V368); asked why he became a policeman, he wants to keep 元木町 peaceful as the cherry-driven population grows. She gives her real name 新村夏菜; he tells her of the case four years ago: the suspect named 新村 vanished with a child; 荒田 was searched in vain. Kana realises the suspect is 叔母さん, whom the settlement hides (叔母さん and 春花ちゃん are on the 山籠); she says she knows nothing.
  - 20: a soba shop run by an old woman who calls Ise 大ちゃん and teases him; Ise on runaways: they all want a 居場所; Kana: she has one but cannot go home. Her backstory (20:97-135): when she started middle school Papa told her to report her first period at once; her sister, at 10, went mad and tried to kill their mother, was confined under the secret base, and her eyes turned from green-and-blue to both blue; Papa can cure the urge only after she has felt it toward Mama; she ran rather than face it. Spring soba with a mountain broth.
  - 26: in the car she teases Ise as a lolicon ("美少女愛好家"); his phone wallpaper is a small twin-tailed girl "今年で20歳"; she phones Papa (幸太郎) on Ise's phone: she cannot come home yet; Papa says he can cure it, asks her to keep in touch, not to talk to Mama for now; she gets his leave to stay with the officer; she asks Ise to keep her by him; the scene where he cannot say her name (26:170-186).
  - 32: at 元木警察署 Ise lends her an old phone (calls only) and takes personal charge of her. On it Kana asks Papa whether 叔母さん killed someone; Papa: she would learn it back at 荒田; the hiding would make Papa and Mama guilty too; Papa: she should have Mama's danger foresight wherever cherries grow. Ise smiles; they go to a family restaurant (ザッハ, named 56:2).
  - 38: Ise warns her to use a false name there; he tells her the case: 新村春花 (21 if alive), taken by 新村美冬, her adoptive mother; Haruka's birth father 古郡良治 was killed in the 元木町事件, her birth mother 古郡茜 is serving time as the perpetrator of the 女ケ沢市事件; Haruka's sister 古郡なつみ (living at her grandparents') and her junior 五島絵梨奈 are still searching; Kana is shaken: the Arata settlement hides a murderer; Ise explains why he told her. 五島絵梨奈 appears (waiting for her sister); Kana, as 古村秋菜, calls Ise 大ちゃん; her first danger foresight: Goto is the danger; she flees to the toilet and plans minimal answers.
  - 44: Goto probes ("ねえねえ、新村さん"; the pasta portion; the sweat); Kana foresees Goto's next four questions (44:68-71) and changes her answer; Natsumi, a university student who avoids 元木町 until the cherries fall, arrives tonight before 21:00 (her father's 命日 was today); 桃子 fetches Goto; Kana says she will go home tomorrow; Ise: four years ago the two jumped from a five-storey roof and vanished; he leads the case now, mostly alone. A long foresight vision: Ise, Goto and Natsumi drive to 荒田; Papa and Mama arrested for harbouring; Haruka with a knife in front of her mother refuses them all ("私の親は新村栄一郎とここにいるお母さんだけだ"); Goto and Natsumi collapse.
  - 50: Kana reasons toward killing 五島絵梨奈 to stop the search, calling it a test of her foresight; touching the lent phone she foresees it is bugged; she pays with a 2,000-yen bill; Ise walks her to a hotel he booked.
  - 179:0-114: in her room (506) Ise tells her 古郡なつみ has seasonal danger foresight and that Goto phoned her; Kana infers he knows of her plan via the bug; she drops it and decides to get 春花 and 叔母さん out of 荒田; she leaves the phone on the bed at 20:30 and runs to 元木駅 (20:40) for the express to 女ケ沢, planning to run 100 km home.
  - 179:115-197: as passengers get off, a strong wind: her urge is to kill people with Mama's kind of foresight, not Mama; she slashes an unknown woman's neck (Natsumi, who half-dodges); the blood sprays nearby women, who turn 翠眼, gain the foresight and kill one another; Kana, still wanting to kill, flees over the fence.
  - 56 (Ise narrating): at ザッハ he tells Goto everything, including the bugged call; Goto asks him to be Kana's support, since she thinks Kana has fallen for him; sirens: killings at 元木駅; among the fallen women Natsumi, throat cut: "青と……緑の目の子……／ナイフで……"; Goto sends Ise after Kana; hotel room empty, the phone on the bed; he runs shouting "夏菜！".
  - 62: Kana in a phone box calls Papa ("たくさん殺しちゃった"; "予知能力を持つ人の血を浴びたら、みんなおかしくなっちゃうんでしょ？"); a TV report; she resolves that the hiding was wrong and that her own existence is the crime; knife at her throat; someone calls her name; Ise stops her hand and holds her; survivors are still frenzied; she knows the cure; on the phone: "パパ……／自首して".
  - 68: 祀耀805 August 1: 新村美冬 sentenced to death; Kana leaves the 元木町 children's home after a year (Ise visited often); the 804 April 8 case: 3 dead, 7 badly hurt; Papa's ファルシフィカソ cured the survivors; the police called it terrorism by a drug or nerve gas and did not look further (an order from the 警察庁); Kana counted a victim; Haruka and 美冬 were found; her parents arrested, back in autumn. At ザッハ Kana meets Natsumi (neck scar; Natsumi sensed the attack and so survived) and apologises; Natsumi thanks her for bringing Haruka back and forbids self-blame. Kana's 16th birthday: Ise gives a photo frame, then a hair clip for Natsumi (birthday July 29) and a melon for Goto; Kana: "16歳って、結婚できる歳だよ！"; Goto slips out for ten minutes.
  - 74: the drive to 元木駅; Kana: someone called her name at the phone box, only Ise knew it; he never uses her name; Ise: tell your parents a policeman will come to 荒田 in two years, on August 1 (her 18th birthday), "1人の男として"; the doors close as he mouths a two-character word: 夏菜; Goto's envelope holds a photo of the proposal scene ("お幸せに！") for the frame; "伊勢さん……／大好きです！".
  - 173:0-31 (replays of 祀耀800, the route of 00000538-0000057B): Ise and Natsumi at the morgue; "古郡良治さんを殺害した犯人は……新村美冬でほぼ間違いない"; Mifuyu's "行くわよ、春花……"; "五島……春花が……消えちゃった……"; 茜 goes out for air; "よくて自首。最悪の場合は――". 173:32-37 Kana: she knew of a big case in 元木町 but not that it concerned the 新村 family; "あれから4年"; meeting them was fated; she is not unconnected.

Haruka's voice: plain, practical past narration with present-feel thought cells; survival know-how (hot spring, rabbit cooking, reading the sun); sensory checklists in one-word cells (100:12-21; 130:88-102); guilt and self-reproach (76:78-88, 76:160-166); comic asides ("性格最悪だな……" 88:85; "何だ、新キャラか？" 106:93); an epilogue in present feel as a hermit years later (193:115-138)

- events (block order 64, 70, 76, 82, 88, 94, 100, 106, 112, 118, 124, 130, 136, 142, 187, 193; story order 82:0-41 (replays of 800 and 802), 76, 70, 136, 130, 124, 118, 112, 106, 64, 100, 94, 88, 142, 187, 193; the 802 continuation of the route of 000005C1-00000656, in which Natsumi's mother was the 死神 and Haruka moved to the 新村本家 and never wrote):
  - 82:0-41 replays: Ise and the 死神 at Goto's house; the flayed father at the 中央公園; Natsumi's mother unmasked and dying; the parting at 元木駅; "しかし、春花から連絡が来ることは、2度となかった。"; caption "――2年後　祀耀802年四月八日"; Natsumi, after her parents' 三回忌, on the train to 荒田 with Goto. 82:42-46 Haruka: she cut contact to train her weak self; she has been two years on the 山籠.
  - 76 (802 April 7 night, on 糸姫山): the 山籠 is a rite of passage for 荒田 women only, a year of survival; Chigaya and 夏菜 exempt. Right after Haruka came to 荒田 in 800 her grandmother died of a heart attack; digging the grave in the 墓地広場 the villagers found a decomposing body ("おい！　死体が埋まってるぞ！"): Haruka's mother, dead about a month, so her 命日 is set at 800 April 8; a photo of two small girls she always kept. Haruka blames her own weakness, starts the 山籠 at 18 against サクラ's advice ("もうそんな慣習に縛られる必要はないわ"), switches her phone off, and will stay until her 20th birthday (April 30), when サクラ (now 大魔女) promised to tell her something. A memory of Goto on the Buddhist hells (等活地獄, 阿鼻地獄 / 無間地獄) and 追善供養 over ozoni she made Haruka pay for; Haruka prays for her mother's soul.
  - 70 (April 8 morning): the secret hot spring 糸姫温泉 (named by her), rabbit meat cooked in the spring; she runs to the settlement in 45 minutes; nobody wears rite dress, the 集会場 is empty at 10:00 though the 三回忌 was due; Chigaya (both eyes blue implied at 124:188) appears silently behind her on the roof ("ん……やっぱり春花。ずいぶん髪が伸びたわね") and leads her, avoiding eyes, to the ruined hut.
  - 136: Chigaya: the 三回忌 is cancelled; "お母さん" (サクラ) foresaw that on 802 April 8 a threat from outside would destroy the settlement; she watches for strangers and hides Haruka's return; she sends Haruka to watch 荒田駅 (夏菜 watches 豊岳駅). Rain begins; Haruka turns back to ask サクラ herself.
  - 130: at the empty 新村家 she changes, cuts her hair to its old length, charges her phone on the home Wi-Fi: two years of weekly messages from Natsumi and Goto; Natsumi, seeing the read receipt: "今日、私と五島で荒田集落に行こうと思うんだけど、春花は集落にいるの？". Hit from behind as she starts to call, dragged by one masked, robed person into a small three-mat room, left there; back in her room a masked figure in 赤装束 hangs from the ceiling, blood dripping on her phone; cut down, it is a dry mummy, the blood soaked into the robe. She searches the settlement shouting (城崎's house, the next house): nobody.
  - 124 (noon): the phone is dead from the blood. She examines the mummy: a girl about puberty, one eye missing. Chigaya appears, punches her, pins her (she calls Haruka prey and her own violence bullying of the weak), then explains: the 荒田 faith is monotheist; its god, unknown to them till now, is this mummy, "ヴェルジ", a 14-year-old girl who died over 400 years ago; the god has "died twice" (hanged); a threat that kills the goddess has entered. "みんな家の中よ？" though the houses are empty. Haruka goes back to watch the station.
  - 118 (15:00, 荒田駅, rain): 夏菜 appears behind her in a tree, knives out: "ヴェルジ様がよそ者3人組を殺せって言ってたんだけどなあ……"; "ママが言ってた"; she leaves Haruka to watch. Haruka meets Natsumi and Goto off the train; she breaks down in their arms; on the waiting train she tells all. Goto's two years of research: 荒田地区 is a 境界未定地 the government closes to outsiders; ドローガ rumours among scholars; the local faith, provisionally "ヴェルジ教", its goddess a girl who died about 450 years ago; the セレジェイラ聖教 of 女ケ沢市, founded by people from 荒田, worshipped ドローガ; its researcher 篠崎ハジメ changed his name; it collapsed about 70 years ago (Haruka: the classmate she defended came from such a family); 翠眼呪殺. They agree to leave and call the police. The train never leaves: the driver's throat is cut; Natsumi and Goto are gone.
  - 112 (20:00-21:00): after searching the station she sees 幸太郎 and Chigaya carry the robed mummy from the 新村家; inside, a hot pot of stew on the table; Natsumi, free and cheerful, eats from it; asked about Goto: "五島……／って？"; "この集落の女神はヴェルジ様だけ"; offers a syringe of pink liquid from 幸太郎, "女神様の血"; she herself will become a 神使, "女神様の一部". Haruka slaps her; nearly injects herself to save them; "五島はいらない" shows it is not an act; she knocks Natsumi out and goes to find Goto.
  - 106 (night): villagers in 能面 and 赤装束 enter the 集会場; 夏菜 marches Goto in at knifepoint; Natsumi walks in with Chigaya. Rite: Chigaya presides: today is サクラ's 一周忌 and one year since ヴェルジ appeared; サクラ's mummy is unveiled as the goddess 『ブルーシャ』, but gives off no 『恵み』, so she is burned as "出来そこないの女神"; the fire reeks of ファルシフィカソ (someone coated the mummy); in the smoke Haruka grabs Goto; Chigaya catches her knife bare-handed (magnesium carbonate on the palm), sweeps her; 夏菜 strikes five vital points; a figure in a white 般若 mask and white robe (白般若) bursts in, beats both sisters with the back of the blade and signals Haruka to run; she carries Goto out.
  - 64: in a cave with a ladder shaft (夏菜's secret base), Goto's account: two people (colour 茅萱 on one voice) took them off the train; at the settlement Natsumi grew calm and rapt ("この集落は安全…女神様がいるから"); the villagers will make her a 神使 (servant of the goddess, i.e. of the dead). Goto phones the police: a landslide blocks the road, arrival at dawn, perhaps a helicopter to the 墓地広場. Natsumi walks into the cave and collapses.
  - 100: outside, the 白般若 leaves an old book and vanishes. Goto reads it: 女神ヴェルジ was トミ, born with one green eye to pure Japanese parents; in 祀耀340 April a woman turned green-eyed and tried to kill everyone; the natives blamed the blue-eyed Western settlers, a civil war fought on ドローガ, those splashed with the dead's blood turned green-eyed and slaughtered; population 100 to 50; green-eyed killed; トミ (2) spared and revered; at 14 the bloodlust came; she gouged out her green eye, still wanted to kill, and killed herself; she was mummified and named ヴェルジ (Portuguese "green"); two 掟: never use ドローガ; keep worshipping ヴェルジ. Goto's "妄想": generations (first: ドローガ users; second: foresight in cherry season; third: green eyes), a fourth generation, the 荒田桜 as a parasite that uses ドローガ and blood to find a queen-bee host; to stop it, destroy the mummy and the 荒田桜.
  - 94: Natsumi wakes sane and apologises ("あれは私じゃないの！"): tonight all stay with the mummy until dawn for the 『繁栄の儀』; then all die and are reborn as part of the goddess; someone carried her out and injected a clear liquid. Goto's model: cherries are self-incompatible, ソメイヨシノ clones; the 荒田桜 breeds through humans; a third generation's corpse releases 『恵み』, like pollen; first and second generations become seedbeds; foresight is a fake gift, telepathy through pollen. Haruka goes to watch the 集会場, promising not to go in.
  - 88 (23:00): the 白般若 attacks her lightly and toys with her; she insults it; it sheathes its hatchet and goes. Inside, the villagers lie prostrate under the hanging mummy. 夏菜 comes out crying, sane ("みんな、ずっと前からおかしくなっちゃって……／ママも……殺されちゃって……"); she only went along; Chigaya and 幸太郎 are lost. The villagers walk out barefoot to their homes, cold to the touch. The 白般若 puts a knife to Haruka's throat; 夏菜, blaming her own year of silence, goes home to sit with her father and sister at the end; Haruka means to take her to 元木町.
  - 142 and 187: back in the cave, Goto on real mind-controlling parasites (ロイコクロリディウム, タイワンアリタケ, ハリガネムシ, ギニアワーム): they signal the host's brain, so the hosts may still be alive; Haruka resolves to find the 白般若. It staggers in and falls; Goto unmasks it: Haruka's mother, worn, no pulse, cold; she gives Haruka a clear syringe for 夏菜 ("じきに、翠眼呪殺の年齢になるから"): "私は……神使になることを……選んだ……". A man's voice: Natsumi says "幸太郎さん……？", Haruka "こいつは――"; he: "父親に向かってこいつなんて呼び方はダメだろ？" (colour 春花の父). His account of 祀耀800 April 8: 茜 confronted Mifuyu at the Revo Heights flat, bluffed a police call, Mifuyu fled; the 女神 had sent him; he pushed Mifuyu, injecting ドローガ on the stairs, down the stairs; 茜 took the half-used ドローガ to go to Goto's house for the two girls and was shot dead there; Mifuyu died; he took her to 荒田 by the goddess's power (strength, foresight, teleport, 死者操作); her body was buried to be found so nobody would search, reburied, then revived as a 神使; a year ago she found the ヴェルジ mummy and brought it to サクラ; with too little ドローガ she kept her will and tried to get Haruka out. The villagers, zombie-like, surround the cave; 夏菜 arrives with Achilles tendons cut and an arm gone; the villagers turn on Goto; Chigaya strangles Goto dead, then Haruka.
  - 193 (dawn, helicopter): crows eat 夏菜's body; Goto dead; Natsumi dead in the cave; Mifuyu gone; villagers lie in their houses as pulsing 胚 (Chigaya, 幸太郎, 城崎). Haruka reasons her mother struck and scared her to drive her out. She sets fire to the 集会場 with both mummies (ファルシフィカソ smell again), and leaves before the helicopter lands. Years later she lives alone as a hermit on the mountain with hallucinations of Natsumi, Goto, her mother and 夏菜: "寂しくないよ。"

- events (the inn trip of 00000E95, told from Haruka's side):

  - "ええっと……お父さん、お母さん……" to the Kogori parents; both decline ("今まで通り"); embarrassed, she goes off alone to explore: a wedding on the 3rd floor (the bride's father absent; her own parents decided to marry the day after meeting; her joke about her father once made her mother truly angry), the 2nd-floor restaurants with a stream in the corridor, a banzai party; she guesses who of the three will drink worst.

  - Natsumi lays the dented, bloodied 大魔女 mask (never handed to the police) on Erika's face; burial tomorrow (土葬, 荒田 custom); Goto leaves a letter written two days before Erika died.

## 0000107B  (order 150)
- narrator: 11:0-12 an omniscient historical preface (である, present-feel); from 11:13 the 皇子 (私), first person, past narration
- time markers: "祀耀の元号が始まる1年前の五月二六日――。" 11:0; "皇子――御年29歳。" 11:8; "湊　綱　宗――御年28歳。" 11:9; "祀耀の年号が始まる前年の五月二六日" 11:12; "もう何か月になるのだろうか。" 11:18; "夏を越え、秋を迎え、冬を耐え抜くと" 11:32; "早春の山" 11:147; "祀耀元年四月八日――。" 11:259; "300年以上に渡る平和な時" 11:262
- events:
  - Preface: under 太良聖守's rule, the 帝's third son, the 皇子, issued a 令旨 to the 湊氏 to strike the 太良 clan for confining the 帝; with 湊綱宗 he rose and was crushed; the rebels were thought executed or hunted down on 5/26 of the year before 祀耀.
  - The 皇子, 綱宗 and a handful of 従者 flee from the 西の都 toward the 東の都 for months, wintering in caves; they have survived by eating their dead, by pact; the girl 糸姫, about 10, of the lowest rank, falls ill and offers herself as food; he refuses ("お前は小さいから食べる所なんてない…死ぬんじゃないぞ").
  - A giant deer kills a smaller giant deer: meat for all; a hot spring; 糸姫 recovers overnight; from a rock, a plain of cherry blossom pink under the moon; 糸姫: "皇子様、私、この場所が大好きです！".
  - He decides to stay and be thought dead, so as not to trouble 湊朝寄, head of the 東の都; 綱宗 proposes 天田 ("天の田"); he chooses the unattractive 荒田 ("荒れた田"), "天田も荒田も語感は似ている"; the mountain is named 糸姫山 after her; he gives up his rank and a new name; "1人はみんなのために、みんなは1人のために".
  - 祀耀元年四月八日: a new settlement founded by the man named 新村 and his companions; 300+ years of peace.
- speakers present: the 皇子 (colour 春花), 湊綱宗 (colour なつみ), 糸姫 (colour 五島), unnamed 従者 (colour ?) (V424)

## 00001092  (order 151)
- narrator: 藤吉郎 (オレ; addressed 吉郎 11:27; full name at 000010AA:11:25); past narration, earthy and ambitious, self-exhortation ("オレに……オレにできることは何だ！？　何なんだ！？" 11:78)
- time markers: "あれは、オレが18歳の頃だったと思う。" 11:14; "そんなある日、祀耀309年の一二月。" 11:22; "おそらく、来年の初夏辺りだろう。" 11:52; "あと半年ほど" 11:76; "――祀耀310年初春。" 11:82; "一月だけ" 11:91; "あと三月と言ったところか" 11:149
- events:
  - A farmer's son whose father was a foot soldier, now kitchen steward (台所奉行) of the 麻生田家, praised by his lord (お館様) for cutting costs; he once served far down under 間川基義 (a noble house descended from the 湊氏, where birth counts) and left at about 18.
  - 309/12: his old friend 豊吉, a 間川 retainer, visits in secret and urges him back: 間川 will pass through 麻生田 land toward the 西の都 next early summer, and 麻生田 must fight and fall; he refuses ("間川に戻るくらいなら、麻生田家と一緒に滅びる方がマシだ！").
  - Early 310 he wins a month's leave to seek a food that makes soldiers strong; he marks the hollow 樽狭間 where 間川 must pass, and plans to have merchants feed them there.
  - Alone he crosses 間川 land and the northern mountains; a giant deer rib under the snow; a boar as big as a house charges; his memory ends (11:186).

## 000010AA  (order 152)
- narrator: 藤吉郎 (オレ); past narration; 11:44-51 新村儀之助's account as unquoted cells inside his turn (11:44 ――-led)
- time markers: "一晩眠っていた" 11:33; "10年前にここで行き倒れていた" 11:56; "――祀耀310年六月一二日。" 11:252; "――午前10時。" 11:260; "――13時、樽狭間の地。" 11:270; "薬の効果は1時間ほどです" 11:227
- events:
  - He wakes in a house in 荒田; a Western-looking man, 新村儀之助, the village doctor, has nursed him; a Japanese woman brings food; 儀之助 speaks a foreign tongue ("Clientes para trazer uma refeicao.").
  - 儀之助's account: 荒田 began centuries ago with a noble who fled the 帝-太良 war; isolated, exclusive, it takes no lord and drives off every invader; he himself, from a colony struck by disaster, came by ship to near the 東の都, was rejected, collapsed here ten years ago and was saved; about 20 Westerners live here under Japanese names; "1人はみんなのため、みんなは1人のため".
  - The giant boar's meat is wondrously strengthening; 藤吉郎 confesses his mission; "Traga as drogas."; the house was surrounded by villagers; 儀之助 trusts him for his honesty and his lord's name (a reformer called "うつけ", curious about the West) and gives him a glass jar of about ten pink crystals of ドローガ, made from the local cherries: burn one before battle, the smoke serves 1000 men, the effect lasts an hour, 鬼神 strength; the condition: never speak of 荒田.
  - 310/6/12: 間川 marches with over 20,000 and 平松泰基 (later 戸川保家) against 麻生田; at 13:00 at 樽狭間 he secretly smokes the 麻生田 main force of 2000 before the downpour; soldiers leap 8 尺 and rout 間川; he dreams of ruling after his lord; 間川 is destroyed.

## 000010C3  (order 153)
- narrator: the 内府 (私; "われわれ戸川" 11:283); past narration, calculating, detached
- time markers: "祀耀332年に起こった理性寺の変" 11:8; "四月二〇日にそこで花見が催される予定だ" 11:46; "期限まであと二〇日ほど" 11:48, 11:74; "祀耀340年四月五日――。" 11:75; "祀耀340年四月一一日――。" 11:206; "約束の四月一二日" 11:207; "――四月二〇日の花見に向けて" 11:225; "――花見から5か月後。" 11:266; "1月も経たないうちに" 11:274
- events:
  - The 殿下 once told him: lost on a mountain north of the 東の都, helped by its people, he received 『桜の呪い』 and as "鬼神の王" worked behind 麻生田延永's rise; the 理性寺の変 of 332 was his doing through 蛙吉; the 内府, who once fought him and now serves, resolves never to oppose him.
  - The 殿下, anxious over his young heir, orders five cherry trees from a secret place for his hanami at 吾大寺 on 四月二〇日; the helpers are to be disposed of after.
  - 340/4/5: with ten ruffians and five carts he reaches 荒田: fires, a slaughter, men cut in half; a man without arms and a leg and a man without a lower body fight; the latter, his heart hanging out, warns "ドローガにゃ手ぇ出すんじゃねえぞ……／俺みたくなるぞ……" and dies; bloodied villagers watch dully; five cherries are dug up and taken.
  - 4/11 at 吾大寺; the helpers are poisoned and burned as fertiliser, reported dead of disease; the lavish hanami (about 1300 guests); he reasons that ドローガ means a drug, that the 殿下 used it at 樽狭間 (where he saw a herd of 鬼神), and that it was meant for the heir.
  - Within a month all five cherries shrivel "like dried mushrooms"; the 殿下 goes mad and dies five months after the hanami; "これからは、われわれ戸川が天下を治めるのだ"; "ドローガには――／決して手を出すな。"
- speakers present: the 内府 (colour 春花の母), the 殿下 (colour なつみの父), ten unnamed ruffians (colour ?), two unnamed 荒田 warriors (11:126, 11:135-136, 11:142-145, 11:160-162, colour ?)

## 000010DD  (order 154)
- narrator: 新村栄一郎 (僕; named 栄一郎 by サクラ 11:45 and 幸太郎 11:87; his lines drawn in the colour 春花の父, speaker by colour); reflective past narration, self-lacerating ("僕は、最低な奴だ……。" 11:210); 僕 switches to 俺 under the drug (11:268-279) and back to 僕 at 11:283
- time markers: "中学二年生の頃" 11:12; "それから数日後、僕が中三になった春のことだった" 11:105; "僕は春休みが終わる前に" 11:118; "今から500年ほど前" 11:120; "祀耀310年" 11:130; "数か月の入院" 11:318; "僕は県外の高校で" 11:345
- events:
  - He, his twin 兄貴 (幸太郎) and サクラ, all the same age, grew up together in 荒田; in his second year of middle school サクラ is bullied savagely for her looks by delinquents; they make him watch so that he cannot bear witness; he stays silent; サクラ tells the adults to keep out; 幸太郎 breaks the gang from inside (they end in juvenile detention).
  - Spring: 幸太郎 warns that the head of 雷怨猫達, a gang from the next city, has fallen for サクラ and dumped his girlfriend, who may set her レディース on サクラ; "俺は、俺にしかできないことをやる。／栄一郎は、栄一郎にしかできないことをやってくれ"; he hints サクラ likes 栄一郎.
  - In the 蔵書, with a dictionary of old Japanese, he reads that about 500 years ago about 20 Westerners led by セレ reached 荒田; セレ, renamed 新村儀之助, made a drug from the local cherry roots and proved it through a lord who struck down 間川基義 in 祀耀310; later セレジェイラ聖教, an offshoot of 荒田, took cherries to study ドローガ; one cherry, the 新村本家 tree, remains.
  - He boils the roots three days in the cave under the secret base (a pink layer = the ドローガ stock), shows サクラ; she begs him not to ("私のために無茶はしないで", "もし私がここから逃げ出したら……一緒に来てくれる？"); he refuses her hand, sends her out, heats the pink liquid into crystals and inhales the smoke alone.
  - Manic ("今の僕なら――／俺なら――。"), he crushes a stone in his hand and cracks the rock wall with a kick; his own leg snaps, bone through the skin, blood pulsing over サクラ in the dark, narrow cave.
  - Months in hospital, a lasting limp; サクラ tells everyone he fell from a tree and hid the tools; 雷怨猫達 dissolved on its own; he decides never to return, asks his mother (母さん) for a high school out of the prefecture, in 元木町 of 佐波県, a town full of cherries, and asks her not to tell サクラ; "桜よりももっときれいな花を、見つけたい。"
- speakers present: 新村栄一郎 (colour 春花の父), 新村幸太郎 as a boy (colour 幸太郎), サクラ (colour サクラ), 新村エリカ (colour エリカ)
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 雷怨猫達, レディース, 少年院, 蔵書, 古語辞典, 隔世遺伝, セレ, 甕, 原液, 結晶, 躁状態, 鬱状態, 縫合手術

## 000010F5  (order 155)
- narrator: サクラ (私; colour サクラ on her lines); a dream of her high-school days (8:0-88), then 祀耀790 (8:89 on); gentle, self-questioning past narration with present-feel thought cells ("何を考えているのだろう、私は。" 8:62)
- time markers: "高校に上がった私たちは、春の田植え" 8:10; "高校卒業後に結婚した" 8:108; "しかし、2年前――。／栄一郎は事故で死んでしまった。" 8:111-112; "去年の夏に生まれた夏菜" 8:97; "茅萱ももうすぐ11歳" 8:151; "春の暖かな日差し" 8:133; "――その日の夕方" 8:177; "四月八日を過ぎる頃には" 8:348
- events:
  - Dream: in high school, planting rice, 幸太郎 says 栄一郎 is well; asks if she still loves him ("好きよ。今でも"); later proposes ("俺たちさ……／結婚しようか", "俺はお前を幸せにできる", "俺が幸せになりたいから……").
  - She wakes on the veranda (Chigaya: "ん……お母さん、どうしたの？"); she married 幸太郎 after high school; 栄一郎 married 美冬ちゃん and died two years ago; 美冬ちゃん and the adopted 春花 live hand to mouth in 女ケ沢市, 春花 bullied; the 掟 bars their moving to 荒田; she has not dared ask the 大魔女 (お義母さん).
  - Hearing 城崎さん forge, she decides to send 美冬ちゃん two sets of hatchet and mace, 能面 and 赤装束 from the 地下倉庫 (in paulownia boxes) to sell, with a letter: not to kill with them (美冬ちゃん had asked "春花をいじめる子供を殺せないか？"); she tells 幸太郎, who doubts it will be enough.
  - In the dark upstairs 三畳間, doing 幸太郎's foresight, she sees 茅萱 coming to kill her; 茅萱 enters ("ん……お母さん、今度の三回忌って――"), clutches her head ("うがががが……！"), "お母さん……／死ね……／死んで……！", knife in hand; 幸太郎 holds her; サクラ flees; the 大魔女 knocks 茅萱 out at a vital point and has her hidden in the 地下倉庫; サクラ is to play "いつも通りの新村家".
  - 幸太郎 finds 翠眼呪殺 and ドローガ in the 蔵書: ファルシフィカソ may cure 茅萱, but was never injected into a person; each of them offers to test it; he asks whether 栄一郎 made ドローガ and whether she touched his blood (his view: that gave her the foresight); they keep it from the 大魔女; he will ask "ドローガを使ってくれる人" to try ドローガ with ファルシフィカソ as its antidote ("お前はそこまで知る必要ない").
  - The test fails: 美冬ちゃん in 女ケ沢 never uses the ドローガ; after April 8 茅萱 is failing ("お母さん……／死んで……／苦しい……"); サクラ secretly injects herself with ファルシフィカソ in the secret base, to give up her foresight in the hope that 茅萱's frenzy stops.
- speakers present: サクラ (colour サクラ), 新村幸太郎 (colour 幸太郎), 新村茅萱 aged 10 (colour 茅萱), 新村エリカ (colour エリカ); 夏菜 as a sleeping baby (no line)

## 0000110D  (order 156)
- narrator: 新村茅萱 (私; a university student in Tokyo, alone at a game, recalling 祀耀790 aged 10); past narration for the memory, present-feel in the frame; bare cells of feeling ("私は……春が嫌い。" 24:138)
- time markers: "10年前の祀耀790年四月一二日――。" 8:28; "栄一郎叔父さんの三回忌が終わってから" 8:29; "私が目を覚まして5日後の祀耀790年四月一七日――。" 8:72; "もうすぐ11歳になる私" 8:49; "当時10歳の私" 24:21; TV "今日東京市は…" 24:124, "今日は東京市でも、早咲きの桜が咲き始めました！" 24:129
- events:
  - Frame: stuck in a video game in her room in Tokyo, she recalls the frenzy of 790: the urge to stab her mother, confinement in the 地下倉庫, the grandmother at her side; the frenzy stopped after some days; she woke on 790 April 12, after 栄一郎叔父さん's 三回忌; her mother holds her and she weeps.
  - 790 April 17: 叔母さん and 春花 (7) come again; 春花's long hair is cut short and she flinches when 茅萱 touches her head ("あ、痛！"), evasive about a wound.
  - From the cherry branch 茅萱 sees her mother run toward the stream, 幸太郎 and the grandmother send the villagers searching; 叔母さん, trembling, leaves at once with 春花 in the family car ("おおお母さんはちょっとビックリしたことがあったみたいで").
  - With baby 夏菜 in a sling she follows; a voice she cannot place says "あっちの方にいるよ" / "ほら、あっちの方だよ"; at the cliff above the stream the grandmother holds her mother back, catches her wrist as she falls, and fails; サクラ dies on the rocks.
  - Hidden, 茅萱 watches her father 幸太郎 behead the body with a hatchet and tell the grandmother "母さん、俺は見てたよ。／サクラを突き落しただろ？": the beheading will make it a stranger's murder; the grandmother answers "私が殺したも同然だね……"; he is to hide the hatchet.
  - 茅萱 carries 夏菜 home crying; back in the frame: game over; the TV forecasts strong wind, pollen and early cherries; "私の1番嫌いな季節がやってくる。…お母さんを殺したくなった季節。"
- speakers present: 新村茅萱 (colour 茅萱), 新村エリカ (colour エリカ), サクラ (colour サクラ), 春花 aged 7 (colour 春花), 新村幸太郎 (colour 幸太郎), おばさん (新村美冬, colour 春花の母), 夏菜 as a baby (colour 夏菜: "んまま？" 8:148, "うう……／うぎゅううう……" 24:102-103); an unseen voice (12:0, 20:0, colour ?); a TV announcer (24:124-129, colour ?)

  - Hung over, 茅萱 takes a call from 美冬叔母さん, in Tokyo on business, who invites her to lunch at 『プリマベラ』 near her flat (the 本店 is in 元木町, where she and 茜 often went).
  - Her record: the tennis-circle コンパ, drinking games, men scared off by her wild streak, a three-digit losing streak; she has woken in a gutter and once floating in the 隅田川; no man has ever asked her out twice.
  - Over an afternoon-tea tower she asks "私って……／女としてダメかな……"; 叔母さん: be liked (change) or find a boyfriend (stay as you are), enjoy the meetings, stop trying so hard; her fault is drink ("酒癖……"); she eats curry and omelette rice besides; she is a big eater with a strong arm.

- time markers: "3時間も" 8:0; "10年前に" 8:11; "9年前は確か……" 8:13; "祀耀791年から795年にかけての春。" 8:45; "祀耀795年で" 8:46; "祀耀796年からの呪殺" 8:50-51; "4月に高校生になるらしい" 8:75; "四月八日" 8:145
- events:
  - She cancels the evening's コンパ with ハナちゃん by phone.
  - Confession (narration): nine years ago, aged 11, she overheard 城崎 and his wife 百合子 quarrel: 百合子 would go to the police about 幸太郎 ("新村家がサクラちゃんを殺した犯人をかばってるってことでしょ！？"); that night she stabbed 百合子 in the throat; one killing each April 791-795, five in all, timed to 荒田's April deaths, called 呪殺; the 796-onward killings are not hers.
  - 叔母さん's message sends photos (curly-quoted, with "(^-^)"); 五島絵梨奈, small and doll-like, sharp; 古郡なつみ's photo shocks her: in '気' she is like her mother.
  - At the mirror in the spring wind she breathes hard: her face grows like her mother's; her blue-and-green odd eyes (夏菜 the same); since 10 she has had seasonal danger foresight, without her mother's dark-narrow condition, and the urge to kill her mother; she has told no one.
  - Her first foresight this year: she dies at 荒田 on April 8 (back cut open, cut in two, or stabbed by 翠眼 春花), with many others; her power fails at 荒田, where cherries are almost gone, so なつみ's may too.
  - Choice: confess her crimes to 叔母さん to learn なつみ's secret, or keep silent.
- speakers present: 新村茅萱 (colour 茅萱; a phone call 8:5-8); in memory 城崎 and 百合子 (8:18-28, colour ?, attributed by content: 百合子 names "あなた" and "明日私が警察に行ってくる", 城崎 names 百合子); おばさん by message (8:72, colour 春花の母)
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: ハナちゃん, 百合子, 城崎夫人, 祀耀791年, 祀耀795年, 祀耀796年, 魔女の力
- voice/relationship changes: 城崎→百合子 百合子; 百合子→城崎 あなた; 城崎→幸太郎 幸太郎君 (3p); 百合子→サクラ サクラちゃん (3p), →エリカ 大魔女様 (3p); 茅萱↔ハナちゃん; rows added
- WITHHELD (author): 8:51 who killed from 796 on: unknown to her.
- NOTE (structural): 8:72 message in ASCII straight double quotes with the emoticon "(^-^)" (V441); 8:139 "（魔女の力）" full-width parentheses (V252 family); 8:141-142 ・ bullets (BULLET GLYPH rule); 8:114-115 "私は荒田集落で――／死ぬ。" two cells; typo V452.
- resolves from earlier files: "00000D59:11:31-32" 城崎's wife the first 呪殺 victim -> killed by 茅萱, aged 11, with a knife to the throat, to stop her going to the police (8:13-39); her name is 百合子. ## 00001184  (order 161)
- narrator: 新村茅萱 (私); past narration of her account, then an interior allegory: a scale (天秤) on her back that talks to her
- time markers: "祀耀791年から795年にかけて" 8:16; "10年前" 8:18; "この9年間" 24:1; "あと九日で十三回忌が始まる" 24:31
- events:
  - 茅萱 tells all: 幸太郎 beheaded サクラ because he thinks the grandmother pushed her; the grandmother never denied it; 叔母さん asks how 茅萱 found them on a stream kilometres long with a baby; she remembers a voice ("あっちの方にいるよ", replayed as 12:0) and shouts "あっちの方！！".
  - 叔母さん: how could the grandmother, pulling サクラ up, look like pushing? and 幸太郎 appeared just after the fall, as if he knew; perhaps it truly is "呪いらしきもの".
  - 茅萱 reveals her own power; 叔母さん is not surprised in サクラ's child; なつみ is not サクラ's child: "私、出産に立ち会ったもの".
  - They list the facts (her frenzy, サクラ's death the day 叔母さん and 春花 came, 百合子 the next year, five in all, the last four years not hers); who else could kill like her? only 夏菜, who has no motive and was 6 at the first unexplained one.
  - 叔母さん: the 呪殺 victims' heads were crushed with a blunt weapon ("頭を鈍器で砕かれていたの"); 茅萱 stabbed hers; the scale on her back taunts her ("それ、本当に君が殺したの？　夢だったんじゃないの？"); her verdict on herself is "空。".
  - She asks to meet 五島 soon, before the 十三回忌 in nine days, and never to meet なつみ (fear of the urge); 叔母さん will arrange it.
- speakers present: 新村茅萱 (colour 茅萱), おばさん (新村美冬, colour 春花の母); the replayed voice (12:0, colour ?); the scale's voice in her head (24:5-14, colour ?)
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 天秤, 托卵, 呪いらしきもの
- voice/relationship changes: the scale→茅萱 君, self 僕; row added
- WITHHELD (author): 12:0 the voice's speaker: still unattributed (V433); 16:31 "まるで、お母さんが落ちるのを知っていたかのような――。" (幸太郎's knowledge) left open.
- NOTE (structural): 12:0 replays 0000110D:12:0 byte for byte (REPLAY, first line 「あっちの方にいるよ」; identical English); 16:0 "「あっちの方！！」" and 20:0 "「頭を鈍器で砕かれていたの」" each alone in its block; 24:5-14 the scale's lines in 「」 (colour ?, V439); 24:22 "空。" one-word cell.
- resolves from earlier files: "00000B0F:11:31-36" (killings went on during 幸太郎's confinement) -> per 美冬 every 呪殺 victim died of a crushed skull, not a stab (20:0; 24:23-27), against 茅萱's memory; unresolved which is true. ACTION: revisit n

## 0000119C  (order 162)
- narrator: 新村茅萱 (私); past narration, flustered and indignant inner voice ("いやいや待て待て、このババアは何言ってるんだ……！" 8:73), then attentive
- time markers: "祀耀800年四月一日――。／十三回忌まで1週間となった。" 8:0-1; "昨日で春休みが終わり" 8:2; "今日、五島という子が東京に来るそうだ" 8:3; "今夜早速歓迎コンパ" 8:5
- events:
  - She turns down ハナちゃん's welcome party and goes to the 銀座 料亭; 五島 and 叔母さん are already feasting (五島 misnames each dish; 叔母さん corrects her with a laugh).
  - 茅萱 greets her formally; 五島: "元木町の天才少女五島絵梨奈、東京に降臨です！"; she needles 茅萱 ("もしかして茅萱さん、少食ですかあ？") until 茅萱 slams the table and speaks plainly; 五島: that was to ease her nerves; she asks leave to call her チガ姉; holding her hand, 五島 studies her with unsmiling eyes.
  - 五島's octopus lecture (why it turns red when boiled, its intelligence, its two-year life): some things show only when heated; then "じゃあ、作戦開始としますか".
  - Her thesis: do not think within common sense; every theory contradicts (human, animal, 茅萱 as culprit), so the contradiction is the answer: something outside common sense, as ドローガ would have hidden the 女ケ沢 killings; 荒田 must hold a secret no villager knows, "『明確な意思を持った者』"; foresight (なつみ's "新村栄一郎さんの声") needs a 『何者か』 that can do telepathy and lend power; the culprit is 『危険を知らせる者』, the 矛盾の支配者; a curse of サクラ is "99%ありえません"; ultrasound was once telepathy.
  - They eat and talk; 五島 lets slip that 叔母さん called 茅萱 a heavy drinker.
- speakers present: 新村茅萱 (colour 茅萱), 五島 (colour 五島), おばさん (新村美冬, colour 春花の母), ハナちゃん (8:4-5, 8:9, colour ?), a 仲居 (8:18-25, colour ?)
- new terms added to GLOSSARY: 祀耀800年四月一日, 歓迎コンパ, 季節の御膳, フグと和牛の特選御膳, 漱石枕流, 嫋やか, アスタキサンチン, カロテノプロテイン, カロテノイド, 無脊椎動物, 脳筋, 何者か, 矛盾の支配者, 危険を知らせる者, 超常現象, 超音波
- voice/relationship changes: 五島→茅萱 茅萱さん -> チガ姉 (asks leave, 8:93; event); 茅萱→五島 五島さん (narration) -> 五島ちゃん (slip 8:78, then steady); 茅萱 formal 丁寧 at the greeting, then タメ口; 茅萱→叔母さん このババア (thought); 五島→おばさん おばさん (3p); rows added

- NOTE (structural): 8:88 "39800円（税抜、サービス料抜、ドリンク別）" full-width parentheses (V252 family); 8:226 "・テレパシーができる⏎・力を分け与える⏎という能力が必須となります" ・ bullets with ⏎ inside a quoted turn; 8:250 "99%" ASCII percent sign; 8:130-132, 8:141-145 long lecture turns across cells; typo V454.

## 000011B4  (order 163)
- narrator: 新村茅萱 (私); on the train to 荒田 on April 5, recalling April 1; warm, grateful past narration; resolution at the close ("それが、五島ちゃんから受け取ったバトンだ。" 8:248)
- time markers: "私は四月五日――十三回忌の3日前に荒田集落へ行くことにした。" 8:8; "当初は四月七日に到着予定" 8:9; "エレベーターまで30分待ち" 8:92; "祀耀790年四月十七日" 8:124; "あと三日――十三回忌が始まるまでに" 8:247
- events:
  - Flashback to April 1: 五島 filches 茅萱's fugu karaage and beef nigiri ("作戦成功です！"); 叔母さん watches, pays, and leaves for her company's branch; 五島 takes 茅萱 to 東京スカイツリー (via 宝町駅, 浅草線, 押上駅) so they can talk in the queue.
  - Six questions in the 5W1H pattern (What/Who/How/When/Where/Why); 茅萱 cannot answer "なぜ"; 五島: that is the 『根』 of the truth; doubt everything she saw, heard or concluded; ask 『本当にそうだろうか』 『なぜそうなのだろうか』.
  - 五島: go to 荒田 early and look for what no one knows (old books, people; she will translate photos); the 『何者か』 lets the seers hear voices because it profits; distrust even the foresight.
  - At the 450 m deck 五島 is delighted; she helps for 新村先輩's sake; 茅萱 is grateful; the train enters the tunnel beyond which there are almost no cherries.

## 000011CC  (order 164)
- narrator: 春花 (私); plain past narration of the trip with present-feel thought ("今回ばかりは、なつみの予知やアドバイスを鵜呑みにするわけにはいかない。" 8:110); an ominous authorial close ("これらは、『何者か』の意思の欠片に過ぎなかった。" 8:227)
- time markers: "明日、お父さんの十三回忌が荒田集落で催される。" 8:0; "チガ姉を最後に見たのは、昨日の夕方" 8:10; "その前日である一昨日の四月五日" 8:22; "昨日の夜" 8:82 (so the file is 祀耀800 April 7)
- events:
  - On the train to 荒田 with なつみ and 五島: チガ姉 has been missing since yesterday evening; 五島 blames herself for sending her early; 春花 and なつみ reassure her.
  - Flashback to April 5: チガ姉 phones 春花 and tells her everything (the 呪殺, 幸太郎 beheading サクラ, her five killings and the doubt about them, 五島's 『何者か』); her foresight fails at 荒田 this year, so meeting なつみ is safe; only 叔母さん, 五島, 春花 and なつみ's parents know of the inquiry; なつみ's foresight said her parents must not go, so なつみ and 五島 come instead; 春花 points out the contradiction of a self-believed killer solving her own case; チガ姉 hangs up to check something ("私自身が矛盾している……？").
  - 五島 reports チガ姉's photos: 『翠眼呪殺』: a girl born with green eyes, after her first period, feels a strong urge to kill green-eyed girls or those with foresight; bleeding men too; 400+ years ago the villagers killed each other.
  - At 豊岳駅, the last change, なつみ listens to the wind: only noise, as if jammed; 五島 proposes going home; なつみ's father phones ("無理せず帰って来なさい"); なつみ says she will go alone, then, calmer, "元木町に……／帰ろうか"; they agree to turn back.
  - A huge trumpet-like, grinding sound shakes them (五島: "ストレンジサウンド……／アポカリプティックサウンド……"); the line is suspended: a full cherry tree has grown up through the track, scattering petals.
  - なつみ, dazed, turns her head "まるで人形のように幾何学的な動きで": "行くしかないよ、荒田集落に……" and "このままだと、なくなっちゃうよ？　元木町が"; 五島: "この事件は常識ではありえないことが背景なんです".

## 000011E3  (order 165)
- narrator: 春花 (私); plain past narration, practical, resolving in short cells at the end ("だから、私も行く。" 8:214; "必ずお前の正体を暴いて、みんなを助け出してやる！" 8:226)
- time markers: "祀耀737年三月三〇日の新聞だ。" 8:57; "この祀耀737年を境に" 8:68; "祀耀790年からは" 8:69; "60年以上前に" 8:85; "祀耀747年四月下旬" 8:122; "帰りも予定通り、四月九日だ！" 8:215
- events:
  - 美冬 fetches them from 豊岳駅 by car. 五島: the 『何者か』 exists and controls the cherries: it grew the tree to stop the train and showed なつみ a vision to bring them to 荒田; she names it 『ネクロ』, short for ネクロマンサー ("英語で呪術師のことをネクロマンサーと言います").
  - 美冬 recalls that the 御神木 in 中央公園 appeared suddenly before her birth; 五島 finds a 祀耀737年三月三〇日 article: 金井桂子 found a 20 m cherry in full bloom; 美冬: "その金井桂子は私の祖母よ"; 春花: "私のひいばあちゃんか"; 元木町's population and fame grew from 737.
  - The 御神木's variety is unknown; the legend モトキザクラ様 (a kimono-clad yokai giving luck or ruin) is old; in late April 祀耀747 five public researchers died in their lodging of crushed skulls, and pressure from somewhere stopped the research; 五島 guesses the government covered it with the urban legend; the truth must have been in paper records from before 737, that is books, which チガ姉 was reading.
  - 美冬 brakes: too dangerous, go home; 春花 refuses (元木町, 荒田, チガ姉, なつみ); 春花 shouts なつみ down; 茜 phones; after the call 美冬 relays 茜 and 良治's view and 茜's words 『あんたはそろそろ子離れして、旅をさせる時期なんじゃないか』; all three insist; "さすが！　私と栄一郎さんの娘ね！"; she floors the pedal.
- speakers present: 春花, 五島, なつみ, おばさん (新村美冬, colour 春花の母); 茜's words quoted by 美冬 (8:205, colour なつみの母); the headline (8:58, colour ?)

- events:

  - 五島 reads 『セレジェイラの教え』, a scripture in old Japanese at least 400 years old: セレ様's family ran a colony for generations; there huge trees and bewitching flowers appeared, the dead rose at the 『猛き音』, and he fled by ship to 荒田, took the name 新村儀之助 and taught the villagers.
  - The fuller account: the native trees they called ボニータ ("美しい") turned monstrous with an earthquake and a trumpet sound; the dead slaughtered people; four generals called 神使 and their demon king アバドン came from a sulphur volcano; allies turned and killed each other in suspicion; he chose to trust those on the ship.

  - The sound comes again; the green trees wither at once (the ナラタケ trait); "死月妖花が本格的に動き出したようだ。"

  - Her summary: spores remain in her, 夏菜 and なつみちゃん; 荒田桜 is a mushroom of a vast fungus; it uses humans, perhaps to breed.

  - 『死月妖花の生態』, by the セレジェイラ聖教 (a 女ケ沢市 faith that faded in the 730s; チガ姉 looks it up): experiments on a condemned man and two subjects made a first, second and third generation; pollen gave (弐) foresight and drove (参) mad, and (参) was shot; (壱) and (弐), kept with the corpse, grew rapt ("もうすぐ土に還る"); spores from the corpse joined the spores in them: the fourth generation; a year later both died in spring with 3 m cherries growing from their chests; the life cycle; a mummified third-generation girl lies at 荒田, and if she surfaces, users are charmed and die as cherries a year later.

  - Reasoning it senses but cannot read thoughts, she starts a phone recording and writes a memo without looking (the intended text given clean, 8:27-51); she realises she miswrote the part on intelligence and cannot fix it; she hides the memo among the books.
  - In 『ドローガの精製法』 she finds 桔梗's 735 note: ファルシフィカソ suppresses the signs of 呪殺; it is being made under the secret base for tomorrow.
  - Barefoot steps; "誰！？"; she draws a knife, recalls her father beheading her mother; "茅萱……": her mother in a white, angel-like robe, face unmarred; チガ姉 refuses the illusion, glares; her mother's eyes flick behind her; she is struck from behind; "さあ、栄一郎、運びましょう" / "よし、サクラはそっちを持って".
  - Carried and nearly senseless, she thinks the books were hidden to prevent exactly this; taken out of the 蔵 and into a freshly dug tunnel; the two know what she sees and hears; they take her "女神様のところ".
  - Her bluff about ファルシフィカソ fails ("あれは小さな芽を枯らすだけよ？"); she breaks free with her second knife; 栄一郎 shows an unmarked neck: they are dead and cannot be harmed; サクラ strikes her down; hyphae bind her; they say the minds are the 女神's, the owners dead ten years.
  - Flailing, she drops the knife and it cuts her arm; at her blood both clutch their heads and collapse, and the hyphae thrash and let go.

  - 92-82: 祀耀815 April, 茜, 15 years into a life sentence for the 女ケ沢市事件 at 佐波刑務所 (皆上市), is bussed to a new 元木刑務所; a young officer, 山垣, is kind; an earthquake and a trumpet-like sound overturn the bus; all others are dead hours later; the bus burns; giant cherries tower over 元木市 and petals fall; handcuffed, she drives an abandoned car into the city: everyone indoors dead without wounds.
  - 76-70: outdoors the dead are bare bones in red fluid; she opens なつみ's flat 408 with the family code (なつみ's birthday reversed) and remembers a convenience-store supper with small なつみ; なつみ appears: "お母さん、お帰り"; radio: an intensity-7 quake, "toxic gas", giant plants; なつみ proposes going to 荒田 to find 春花 and hide 茜 there, calling at 五島's first.
  - 64-58: 五島, alive in a sealed top-floor flat, head of a 国際研究所: the cherries are a fungus's fruiting bodies; root spores give ドローガ; fresh blood passes it on; 茜 (a user) and なつみ (second generation, via 栄一郎) survive, she would die outside; the air holds spores, not gas; flies and crows live, mammals die; they stay to help her.
  - 52-40: at 五島's request 茜 films petals dissolving a corpse at ザッハ; a lone line of footprints leads back to the giant 御神木 in 中央公園 and ends; 五島 spots 春花 at the window in the video; 茜 finds なつみ collapsed under petals; hiding in a convenience store they hear a woman's voice offering rescue (V558).
  - 34-14: the video shows 美冬 in a seamless white robe like the 赤装束; the 官房長官 (中嶋長司) says the special unit will not rescue, which 五島 reads as a warning; 五島: the 747 killings of a 御神木 research group (the ナナシナ事件, V557) were covered by the invented モトキザクラ様 legend in the magazine 『M』; the tree has a will; the killers are people 荒田桜 controls; 春花 rings the intercom and collapses; なつみ runs down.
  - 8: 春花, looking 20, believes it is 802: after 美冬 died at 荒田 she did a two-year 山籠 and on 802 April 8 blacked out beside a one-eyed girl's mummy at the 新村本家; 五島's hypothesis: 13 years dormant in 荒田桜, moved underground (or copied) to 元木市.
  - 20-28: 茜 dreams that the girls lead her to a giant cherry where 美冬 waits ("これで4人そろったわ") and ropes bind her: "死月妖花だよ。" (24:0); at dawn both girls are gone; 五島 says she doubts 茜 and 良治 did the 女ケ沢市 killings and will seek a retrial; 茜 recalls that someone killed the first target before she could (28:171-176); gunfire.
  - 193-197: about 15 soldiers in protective suits lie dead; blood-soaked なつみ apologises; 春花 tears out her heart from behind; 春花's account: 美冬 died reaching 荒田; the 女神's 『恵み』 made 春花 a 神使 on 802 April 8; with 茜 the four would be complete: "死月妖花の一部にさ。" (197:0).
  - 201: なつみ lives again; the girls urge 茜 to wish to stay with them; she wishes, something enters her; they decide 五島 must "大地に還って"; the government declares a permanent 100 km exclusion zone; 茜: "とても幸せ。"

## 00002187  (order 378)
- narrator: 桔梗 (私), expository, past; general statements present ("知れば知るほど不思議な薬だ")

- events:
  - ドローガ: refined from the roots of the cherry trees native to 荒田; the crystal is heated and the smoke inhaled; it gives strength "まるで鬼神のような" (8:0-3).
  - with it hunting is easy, so 荒田 never lacks food; most would call it a gift from heaven (8:4-7).
  - 桔梗 holds that everything has causality ("因果律"); a strange thing has a reason, a cause and a result; her work is to find it in the old texts (8:8-13).
  - invented about 400 years ago; nobody knows the details; there must be a record (8:14-16).
  - the library is full of Western ideas: 死神, 魔女; the 大魔女 runs the settlement with several 魔女 assisting; ドローガ is said to mean 『薬』 in the West (8:17-20).
  - 死神 elsewhere guides the living to the other world; here the dead are called 死神; at a 法要 all wear 能面 and 赤装束 and the 大魔女 destroys the dead's clothes; no incense; it resembles exorcising a 死神 (8:21-27).
  - the settlement worships a mummy, the girl ヴェルジ, revered since she saved the settlement about 300 years ago; the odd 法要 may have a meaning (8:28-31).

## 000021A5  (order 383)
- narrator: 桔梗 (私), past

- events:
  - in March 729 a man arrives in a fine car; villagers stir; he opens the 新村家 door: "失礼します！" (colour 五島) (8:0-6).
  - about 35, upright, he salutes: 篠崎ハジメ, sent by the government from 東京 to speak with the leader (8:9-14).
  - she lets him in, partly because the villagers are watching; tea and sweets; she introduces herself as 新村桔梗, the leader (8:15-26).
  - he shows an old letter: "私は辺境の地、荒田から参りました。", signed 『荒田集落代表　新村晃』; memories of 晃 flood back (8:28-38).
  - he says this drug may now serve the country; she remembers her second purpose in marrying in: to find the truth of ドローガ and 呪殺; she wonders whether 晃's death was suicide (8:39-52).

  - 春花 wakes in a hospital room; 五島: "もう起きないかと思いましたよ！"; "なつみ、死んだんだよな……"; "私が殺した。これは紛れもない事実。" (11:0-10).
  - 五島 would prove an external cause; 春花: no; asks her to rescue a poor girl on 糸姫山 in 荒田, used by a bad one for 800 years (11:11-23); 五島: "天才少女五島絵梨奈にお任せください！" then sobs; 春花's mother cried daily (11:24-32).
  - narration: through 五島, 死月妖花's existence came out; 糸姫 was separated from it and is protected as a state secret; years later life is normal; she was never judged by law; at cherry time she watches the sky alone (11:33-48).
  - with 五島 in April: clouds, wonder, having a heart; 五島 would have accepted her staying asleep in a world where 古郡先輩 lives; 春花 came back for 五島 and her mother; her mother is strong (11:49-102).
  - 永劫回帰, glossed in the text as 『一瞬一瞬を大切に生きる』『過去を後悔するより未来へ希望を馳せる』; she faces what she did (11:103-112).
  - the girl of 糸姫山 will soon enter society; 春花 wishes her happiness: "私とあいつって似てるだろ？" (11:113-134).
  - 五島: she is softer, more feminine; 春花: grew her hair; a person in her dream taught her real strength; 五島 treats her at ザッハ, then asks help choosing 夏菜ちゃん's school-entry gift; 春花 wants ミラノポリス's pizza (11:135-166).
  - "なつみ、私は生きているよ" / なつみ's voice (colour なつみ): "うん、春花はもう大丈夫だよ" (11:167-173).
