## 00002444  (order 415)
- narrator: four blocks. 14: 五島 (私; "天才少女と言われる私" 14:111; 古郡先輩 / 新村先輩), past with present-feel analysis. 20: 春花 (私; boyish narration, だろ / な). 26: 五島 again. 32: 春花 again, closing years later in present-feel guilt. No switch sprites in this file; narrators inferred from content.
- time markers: "今から20分ほど前" (14:18); "今はまだ四月六日" (14:47, her wishful thought); "祀耀800年四月八日深夜1時" (14:290); "10分" (14:349); "祀耀800年四月八日……0:30" (20:37); "三月の下旬" (20:34); "この半月くらい" (20:47); "800年前――祀耀元年" (20:410); "3時間後、夜明け" (20:552); "2～3時間後" (26:4); "20分くらい" (26:31); "20年ほど前" (26:400); "5日も経過" / "四月一三日の朝" (32:297-298); "その3ヶ月後" (32:313); "あと15年ほど" (32:248); "祀耀815年" (32:322); "あれから15年" (32:329); "この1週間" (32:328)
- events (block 14, 五島):
  - 五島 hides in a cave, panting; twenty minutes earlier masked villagers burst in while she, 古郡先輩, 新村先輩 and 幸太郎さん discussed the case; they struck 古郡先輩 and チガ姉; 新村先輩 pushed her out; she ran (14:0-37).
  - she believes 古郡先輩 dead; recalls "呪いを持ち込む魔女" and "知花の敵"; the cave has a hemp mat and snack litter; she steps out and a gloved red hand covers her mouth (14:38-115).
  - 城崎健吾 ("俺は君の味方だ") carries her back in; she calls him a murderer; he lays down a knife, offers his neck; she questions him (14:116-198).
  - from his account she finds the story of how the villagers learned of 古郡先輩's power points to 幸太郎さん: 城崎 says サクラ foresaw in dark narrow places in blossom time, but 幸太郎 had said サクラ waited on the cliff for April sickness to make her fall (recalled 14:224-229) (14:199-243).
  - she trusts 城崎: he hid outside the main house; 春花 and 美冬 are unaccounted for; she gives her name ("五島って呼んでください"); he calls her 五島ちゃん and dresses her in a 能面 and 赤装束 (14:244-289).
  - 祀耀800年四月八日深夜1時: walking; 百合子 was his wife and wanted the police the day before she died (14:290-344).
  - scouting, he finds the villagers dead everywhere; they pass the bodies, take his hunting rifle and buggy (14:345-429).
  - on the mountain road two figures: おじさん and おばさん (the 古郡夫妻), come up from the shed by the bugging app; 良治 and 城崎 go to the settlement, 茜 and 五島 take the buggy; 春花 is alive and hiding (14:430-515).
- events (block 20, 春花):
  - 春花 wakes at the foot of a slope in pain; her last memory is her mother in late March saying 茜たち will skip the 十三回忌 and なつみ and 五島 will go (a foresight); her phone says 祀耀800年四月八日 0:30; half a month is gone from her memory (20:0-87).
  - a girl in a red kimono, long hair, one green eye, "糸と言います", lights a fire by friction, knows her normal manner of speech, says she can meet 春花 only now (20:88-176).
  - 糸: 美冬 and 五島 are safe; なつみ is dead, "ただ死んだだけなら、まだよかった"; she herself is the 呪殺 culprit; if 春花 meets なつみ, do not trust her, run (20:177-270).
  - なつみ appears by the fire; 糸 drags 春花 away; 春花 remembers the attack, the flight with 美冬 (barefoot), her fall from a cliff (20:271-356).
  - 糸: the 神使 killed one a year at her order; the villagers' riot killed なつみ, 茅萱 and 夏菜; she has broken with "彼女", the 親玉, "一応は人間"; she has been here since 祀耀元年; 栄一郎 and サクラ are searching too (20:357-421).
  - 良治 and 城崎 find them; 城崎 recognises 糸 from the 糸姫山 legend and a 書簡 ("齢15ほどの赤い着物を着た少女。それが邪悪なる女神である"); 糸 admits it and that she killed his wife; he fires past her and makes her their guard (20:422-536).
  - 良治 phones for help: 五島 already reported it; a small unit in 3 hours, more at dawn; 糸 realises なつみ has gone after 五島 and おばさん; 五島 does not answer; 春花 runs down the road (20:537-579).
- events (block 26, 五島):
  - 五島 calls the police (first-response unit to 荒田駅 in 2-3 hours); 茜 cannot reach 良治 (call blocking while tracking); they ride the buggy toward the station (26:0-33).
  - 五島 reasons: 幸太郎 lied about サクラ's foresight because he expected them to survive; the massacre was one-sided, done by a few with inhuman strength who vanished; a hidden true culprit controls everything (26:34-116).
  - なつみ appears, unhurt and calm, in white; 茜 embraces her; 五島 hears three lies and shouts "嘘だ！！！"; she calls her 古郡先輩's shape and an 異形; 茜 slaps 五島 and leaves on the buggy with なつみ (26:117-236).
  - 春花 arrives exhausted, then 良治, 城崎 and 糸 on 城崎's back; 糸 explains the 神使, ドローガ, the generations; the tree wants four 神使: living and dead first and second generation; 五島 deduces how 幸太郎 and サクラ used the villagers (an imagined scene with 幸太郎, サクラ, 香織, 城崎) (26:237-359).
  - 糸 was linked to なつみ's senses until the attack; she is no longer the 核; the new 核 (アバドン) was taken in about 20 years ago; 糸 calls out to her; a roar and wind; the dead 栄一郎 and サクラ appear, then なつみ carrying the limp 茜; 栄一郎: 母さんと兄貴 are held alive; サクラ: 茅萱 and 夏菜 are luckily dead; 栄一郎 calls on the 女神 (26:360-504).

  - a news report on a quake in 元木市 with toxic gas; 春花 had moved なつみ and 五島 away; in 祀耀815 nearly 200,000 die; she kept the promise and blames herself; "あの時からずっと、あの人が見つかっていないのだ" (32:317-341).

## 0000246E  (order 420)

  - サクラ: the villagers attacked, but 茅萱 and 夏菜 kept all safe; she felled a tree across the road and released a little spore to make them foresee, so they must return; she will not kill 城崎; bring なつみ; with なつみ the 女神 has no weakness and will absorb 荒田; three 神使 (サクラ, 栄一郎, なつみ) and one living first generation needed; any drugged villager would do; use 春花 or anyone as leverage (11:240-293).

  - outside the cave エリカ waits; a hidden passage deep underground leads toward 糸姫山 (rockfalls); he tests her: "俺がサクラの首を切ったこと"; she: "お前、もしかして気づいたのかい？"; the one who beheaded サクラ told her in his shape that he had pushed her and cut off the head to stage a strange murder; "栄一郎か……" / "多分ね"; she slaps him twice, takes a 鉈, will protect なつみ and the settlement: "荒田の男なら落とし前はつけな"; dust from 美冬's car; "1人はみんなのために、みんなは1人のために" (11:302-391).

## 00002473  (order 421)
- narrator: 幸太郎 (俺; inferred), past with present-feel planning; feigned villainy over inner resolve; ends raving
- time markers: "10年前" (11:112); "0.1秒" (11:152); "何日も" (11:156); "5月の連休か夏休み" (11:278); "20年程度" / "8年以内" (11:294); "つい昨夜" (11:295); "800年" (11:303); memories of 茅萱's birth, 夏菜's birth day, 栄一郎's death, サクラ's death "2年後", "その1年後" (11:338-375)
- events:
  - he leads なつみ, 春花, 五島 and 夏菜 deep underground, "to shelter them"; his mother's words have changed him; he weighs 死月妖花's two options (kill everyone, or leave the drugged villagers loose) and hopes in 城崎's police (11:0-39).
  - he says it is a dead end (the way to 糸姫山 passes ヴェルジ's mummy, which サクラ must not find); to deceive サクラ he must deceive all; he plays the villain: "俺がみんなをやったんだよ……！"; 五島: "誰をかばっているんですか？" (11:40-76).
  - he takes ドローガ, knocks 夏菜 out (a deer technique), lifts 春花, sees 五島 has swapped the key; サクラ is a doll in her shape; he aims past 春花 (11:77-127).
  - サクラ's voice in his head (only the second and third generations should hear it): the seal stone is moved; ヴェルジ's spores are making him fourth generation; she threatens to kill everyone in 0.1 seconds if he tells (11:128-152).
  - he reveals 春花 is adopted, なつみ's real sister; plans to break his own arm on the ground; なつみ jumps in front, the blade strikes something hard; 五島 gets them through the door and locks it; エリカ calls from above; he batters the door (11:153-216).
  - the 新村本家 cherry tree falls on him; 栄一郎 ("よう兄貴") and サクラ; hyphae hold him; the girls escape with なつみ and 夏菜 (11:217-249).
  - サクラ and 栄一郎: he refuses to be a 神使; 栄一郎: なつみ will return in the May holidays or summer; 春花 got なつみ's blood and is third generation; at her first period or bleeding in 荒田 she will kill なつみ; the 女神's other wish is to erase 春花 (jealousy); a 神使 lasts about 20 years; 糸姫, judged unfit after 800 years, is held bound; the new 女神 has 荒田 and 元木 blood; they vanish: "さようなら幸太郎、愛しているわ" (11:250-317).
  - voices in his head; memories of 茅萱's birth, of 夏菜's birth day with 茅萱, of 栄一郎's death (a fall from a tree), サクラ's death two years later (head cut off), her return a year later after "あの怪音"; he is rescued, taken away, vowing revenge on "化け物ども" (11:318-392).

## 00002478  (order 422)
- narrator: 茅萱 (私; inferred: "茅萱！" 11:77, "新村茅萱さん" 11:99), past with present-feel reflection; measured

- events:
  - 茅萱 visits her father behind a thick acrylic panel, an officer present; she must learn what happened then and what is coming (11:0-7).
  - he sways, eyes unfocused; his lawyer reports him worsening; he took ドローガ, but tests found nothing: hushed up; the mass arrests in 荒田 went unreported; the visit's condition: no talk of the case (11:8-27).
  - she tells him おばあちゃん died (heart attack) and なつみちゃん died, by 春花, who has been unconscious since; he rages and is held (11:28-55).
  - "ファルシフィカソを……／使え……"; the visit is cut short; he shouts "茅萱！　女神を殺せ！" and "称えろ！" alternately; both are dragged out; she is told not to speak of it, by order of the 警察庁 (11:56-101).
